`AC_STP (nocleo)/README.md`:

```md
# STP-Vanguard — no-CLEO build

This folder contains the no-CLEO client build and the matching Windows x86 server plugin.

For setup, protocol details, scan behavior, limitations, SQL integration, and the project license, see the [repository README](../README.md).

```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/client/diagnostics.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once
#include <Windows.h>
#include <mutex>
#include <string>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include "runtime.hpp"
inline void LogStatus(const char* message) {
    static std::mutex mutex;
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex);
    char path[MAX_PATH]{};
    if (!GetModuleFileNameA(g_Self, path, MAX_PATH)) return;
    auto slash = strrchr(path, '\\'); if (!slash) return;
    *(slash + 1) = '\0';
    std::string filename = std::string(path) + "STP.Vanguard.log";
    auto file = CreateFileA(filename.c_str(), FILE_APPEND_DATA, FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE,
        nullptr, OPEN_ALWAYS, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
    if (file == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;
    SYSTEMTIME time{}; GetLocalTime(&time);
    char line[512]{};
    int count = snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "%04u-%02u-%02u %02u:%02u:%02u %s\r\n",
        time.wYear, time.wMonth, time.wDay, time.wHour, time.wMinute, time.wSecond, message);
    DWORD written = 0;
    if (count > 0) WriteFile(file, line, static_cast<DWORD>((std::min)(count, int(sizeof(line) - 1))), &written, nullptr);
    CloseHandle(file);
}
```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/client/identity.cpp`:

```cpp
// Hardware identity is computed once, independently of networking and scanning.
#include <Windows.h>
#include <wincrypt.h>
#include <comdef.h>
#include <wbemidl.h>
#include <string>
#include <sstream>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <intrin.h>
#include "obfuscate.h"
#include "runtime.hpp"
#pragma comment(lib, "wbemuuid.lib")
std::string GetLocalHWID() {
    std::stringstream raw;
    int cpuInfo[4] = { 0 };
    __cpuid(cpuInfo, 1);
    char cpuHex[17] = { 0 };
    sprintf(cpuHex, AY_OBFUSCATE("%08X%08X"), cpuInfo[3], cpuInfo[0]);
    raw << cpuHex;

    HKEY hKey;
    if (RegOpenKeyExA(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, AY_OBFUSCATE("SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Cryptography"), 0, KEY_READ | KEY_WOW64_64KEY, &hKey) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        char guid[128] = { 0 };
        DWORD size = sizeof(guid);
        if (RegQueryValueExA(hKey, AY_OBFUSCATE("MachineGuid"), NULL, NULL, (BYTE*)guid, &size) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
            std::string guidStr(guid);
            guidStr.erase(std::remove_if(guidStr.begin(), guidStr.end(), [](char c) { return c == '-' || isspace(c); }), guidStr.end());
            if (!guidStr.empty()) raw << guidStr;
        }
        RegCloseKey(hKey);
    }

    char volumePath[4] = "C:\\";
    DWORD serial = 0;
    GetVolumeInformationA(volumePath, NULL, 0, &serial, NULL, NULL, NULL, 0);
    char serialHex[9] = { 0 };
    sprintf(serialHex, "%08X", serial);
    raw << serialHex;

    std::string input = raw.str();
    if (input.empty()) return std::string((const char*)AY_OBFUSCATE("00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000"));

    HCRYPTPROV hProv = 0;
    HCRYPTHASH hHash = 0;
    std::string finalHWID = AY_OBFUSCATE("00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000");
    if (CryptAcquireContext(&hProv, NULL, NULL, PROV_RSA_FULL, CRYPT_VERIFYCONTEXT)) {
        if (CryptCreateHash(hProv, CALG_MD5, 0, 0, &hHash)) {
            CryptHashData(hHash, reinterpret_cast<const BYTE*>(input.c_str()), static_cast<DWORD>(input.length()), 0);
            BYTE hash[16];
            DWORD hashLen = 16;
            if (CryptGetHashParam(hHash, HP_HASHVAL, hash, &hashLen, 0)) {
                char hex[33] = { 0 };
                for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) sprintf(hex + (i * 2), "%02X", hash[i]);
                char formatted[37] = { 0 };
                sprintf(formatted, "%8.8s-%4.4s-%4.4s-%4.4s-%12.12s", hex, hex + 8, hex + 12, hex + 16, hex + 20);
                finalHWID = formatted;
            }
            CryptDestroyHash(hHash);
        }
        CryptReleaseContext(hProv, 0);
    }
    return finalHWID;
}

// =============================================================
// GetLocalHWID_V2() - Persistent Hardware ID (Valorant-style)
// เปลี่ยน Windows ใหม่ก็ไม่หาย ต้องเปลี่ยนเมนบอร์ด/ดิสก์จริง ๆ
// =============================================================
static std::string g_CachedHWID_V2 = "";

std::string GetWMISerial(const std::wstring& wqlQuery, const std::wstring& propertyName)
{
    std::string result;
    HRESULT hres = CoInitializeEx(0, COINIT_MULTITHREADED);
    if (FAILED(hres)) return result;

    hres = CoInitializeSecurity(NULL, -1, NULL, NULL,
        RPC_C_AUTHN_LEVEL_DEFAULT, RPC_C_IMP_LEVEL_IMPERSONATE,
        NULL, EOAC_NONE, NULL);

    IWbemLocator* pLoc = NULL;
    IWbemServices* pSvc = NULL;
    IEnumWbemClassObject* pEnumerator = NULL;

    if (SUCCEEDED(CoCreateInstance(CLSID_WbemLocator, 0, CLSCTX_INPROC_SERVER,
        IID_IWbemLocator, (LPVOID*)&pLoc)))
    {
        if (SUCCEEDED(pLoc->ConnectServer(_bstr_t(L"ROOT\\CIMV2"), NULL, NULL, 0L, 0L, NULL, NULL, &pSvc)))
        {
            CoSetProxyBlanket(pSvc, RPC_C_AUTHN_WINNT, RPC_C_AUTHZ_NONE, NULL,
                RPC_C_AUTHN_LEVEL_CALL, RPC_C_IMP_LEVEL_IMPERSONATE, NULL, EOAC_NONE);

            if (SUCCEEDED(pSvc->ExecQuery(_bstr_t("WQL"), _bstr_t(wqlQuery.c_str()),
                WBEM_FLAG_FORWARD_ONLY | WBEM_FLAG_RETURN_IMMEDIATELY, NULL, &pEnumerator)))
            {
                IWbemClassObject* pclsObj = NULL;
                ULONG uReturn = 0;
                while (pEnumerator)
                {
                    HRESULT hr = pEnumerator->Next(5000, 1, &pclsObj, &uReturn);
                    if (hr != WBEM_S_NO_ERROR || uReturn == 0)
                        break;

                    VARIANT vtProp;
                    VariantInit(&vtProp);
                    if (SUCCEEDED(pclsObj->Get(propertyName.c_str(), 0, &vtProp, 0, 0)) &&
                        vtProp.vt == VT_BSTR && vtProp.bstrVal)
                    {
                        result = _bstr_t(vtProp.bstrVal);
                        VariantClear(&vtProp);
                        pclsObj->Release();
                        break;
                    }
                    VariantClear(&vtProp);
                    pclsObj->Release();
                }
                pEnumerator->Release();
            }
            pSvc->Release();
        }
        pLoc->Release();
    }
    CoUninitialize();
    return result;
}

// =============================================================
// GetLocalHWID_V2() - Persistent Hardware ID (ใช้ UUID จาก Motherboard)
// ลง Windows ใหม่ → ไม่เปลี่ยน
// ฟอร์แมตไดรฟ์ → ไม่เปลี่ยน
// เปลี่ยน CPU/RAM/GPU/SSD → ไม่เปลี่ยน
// เปลี่ยนเมนบอร์ดเท่านั้น → เปลี่ยน
// =============================================================


std::string GetLocalHWID_V2()
{
    if (!g_CachedHWID_V2.empty())
        return g_CachedHWID_V2;

    // ใช้ UUID จาก Win32_ComputerSystemProduct (แน่นที่สุด)
    std::string uuid = GetWMISerial(L"SELECT UUID FROM Win32_ComputerSystemProduct", L"UUID");

    // ป้องกันค่า default หรือว่าง
    if (uuid.empty() ||
        uuid == std::string(AY_OBFUSCATE("Default")) ||
        uuid.find(AY_OBFUSCATE("00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000")) != std::string::npos)
    {
        g_CachedHWID_V2 = AY_OBFUSCATE("00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000");
        return g_CachedHWID_V2;
    }

    // ใช้ UUID เป็น input แล้ว hash เหมือนเดิม (MD5 + GUID format)
    // เพื่อให้ format ตรงกับโค้ดเดิม ไม่ต้องแก้ส่วนอื่น
    std::string input = uuid;

    HCRYPTPROV hProv = 0;
    HCRYPTHASH hHash = 0;
    std::string finalHWID = AY_OBFUSCATE("00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000");

    if (CryptAcquireContext(&hProv, NULL, NULL, PROV_RSA_FULL, CRYPT_VERIFYCONTEXT))
    {
        if (CryptCreateHash(hProv, CALG_MD5, 0, 0, &hHash))
        {
            CryptHashData(hHash, (const BYTE*)input.c_str(), (DWORD)input.length(), 0);

            BYTE hash[16];
            DWORD hashLen = 16;
            if (CryptGetHashParam(hHash, HP_HASHVAL, hash, &hashLen, 0))
            {
                char hex[33] = { 0 };
                for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i)
                    sprintf(hex + (i * 2), AY_OBFUSCATE("%02X"), hash[i]);

                char formatted[37] = { 0 };
                sprintf(formatted,
                    AY_OBFUSCATE("%8.8s-%4.4s-%4.4s-%4.4s-%12.12s"),
                    hex, hex + 8, hex + 12, hex + 16, hex + 20);

                finalHWID = formatted;
            }
            CryptDestroyHash(hHash);
        }
        CryptReleaseContext(hProv, 0);
    }

    g_CachedHWID_V2 = finalHWID;
    return g_CachedHWID_V2;
}


DWORD WINAPI IdentityWorker(LPVOID) {
    try {
        stp::Identity identity;
        identity.type = kClientType;
        identity.hwid = GetLocalHWID();
        identity.hwid2 = GetLocalHWID_V2();
        g_Session.SetIdentity(identity);
    } catch (...) { OutputDebugStringA("STP Vanguard: identity initialization failed\n"); }
    return 0;
}


```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/client/integrity.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once
#include <Windows.h>
#include <wincrypt.h>
#include <array>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>

// SHA-256 of each executable page, captured once after the module has loaded.
// A local integrity check detects later patches; it is not remote attestation.
class CodeIntegrity {
    struct Page { const BYTE* address; DWORD size; std::array<BYTE, 32> hash; };
    std::vector<Page> pages_;
    HCRYPTPROV provider_ = 0;
    std::size_t next_ = 0;
    bool Hash(const BYTE* data, DWORD size, std::array<BYTE, 32>& out) {
        HCRYPTHASH h = 0;
        if (!CryptCreateHash(provider_, CALG_SHA_256, 0, 0, &h)) return false;
        DWORD length = 32;
        bool ok = CryptHashData(h, data, size, 0) && CryptGetHashParam(h, HP_HASHVAL, out.data(), &length, 0);
        CryptDestroyHash(h); return ok;
    }
public:
    ~CodeIntegrity() { if (provider_) CryptReleaseContext(provider_, 0); }
    bool Initialize(HMODULE module) {
        if (!CryptAcquireContextA(&provider_, nullptr, nullptr, PROV_RSA_AES, CRYPT_VERIFYCONTEXT)) return false;
        auto base = reinterpret_cast<const BYTE*>(module);
        auto dos = reinterpret_cast<const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER*>(base);
        auto pe = reinterpret_cast<const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS*>(base + dos->e_lfanew);
        auto section = IMAGE_FIRST_SECTION(pe);
        for (unsigned i = 0; i < pe->FileHeader.NumberOfSections; ++i) {
            if (!(section[i].Characteristics & IMAGE_SCN_MEM_EXECUTE)) continue;
            for (DWORD offset = 0; offset < section[i].Misc.VirtualSize; offset += 4096) {
                Page p{base + section[i].VirtualAddress + offset, (std::min)(DWORD(4096), section[i].Misc.VirtualSize - offset), {}};
                if (!Hash(p.address, p.size, p.hash)) return false;
                pages_.push_back(p);
            }
        }
        return !pages_.empty();
    }
    bool Check() {
        if (pages_.empty()) return false;
        // Eight pages per pass bounds work to 32 KiB.
        for (unsigned i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            auto& p = pages_[next_];
            MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION m{};
            if (!VirtualQuery(p.address, &m, sizeof(m)) || m.State != MEM_COMMIT ||
                (m.Protect & (PAGE_GUARD | PAGE_NOACCESS | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY))) return false;
            std::array<BYTE, 32> hash{};
            if (!Hash(p.address, p.size, hash) || hash != p.hash) return false;
            next_ = (next_ + 1) % pages_.size();
        }
        return true;
    }
};

```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/client/main.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <Windows.h>
#include <string>
#include "network.hpp"
#include "runtime.hpp"
#include "diagnostics.hpp"

static bool IsTargetServer(RakClientInterface* client) {
    if (!client || !client->IsConnected()) return false;
    auto id = client->GetServerID();
    auto ip = client->PlayerIDToDottedIP(id);
    return ip && id.port == 7777 && (!strcmp(ip, "stp-games.com") || !strcmp(ip, "127.0.0.1"));
}

static void NetworkTick() {
    auto client = Network::Instance().GetRakClient();
    bool active = IsTargetServer(client);
    if (!active) {
        if (g_Active.exchange(false)) g_Session.Reset();
        return;
    }
    g_Active = true;
    g_Session.Tick(GetTickCount());
}

static DWORD WINAPI Bootstrap(LPVOID) {
    LogStatus(kClientType == 0 ? "9.11.0 starting: CLEO allowed" : "9.11.0 starting: no CLEO");
    char path[MAX_PATH]{};
    GetModuleFileNameA(g_Self, path, MAX_PATH);
    const char* name = strrchr(path, '\\'); name = name ? name + 1 : path;
    if (_stricmp(name, "STP.Vanguard.asi")) {
        MessageBoxA(nullptr, "Rename the client to STP.Vanguard.asi", "STP Vanguard", MB_OK | MB_ICONERROR); return 0;
    }
    // Pin: callbacks and the scanner must never outlive the module.
    HMODULE pinned = nullptr;
    if (!GetModuleHandleExA(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_PIN,
        reinterpret_cast<LPCSTR>(&Bootstrap), &pinned)) return 0;
    auto& network = Network::Instance();
    g_Session.send = [](const stp::Bytes& b) { return Network::Instance().SendRaw(b); };
    network.QueueReport = [](const void* p, std::size_t n) { return g_Session.Enqueue(p, n); };
    network.OnTick = NetworkTick;
    network.OnDisconnect = [] { g_Active = false; g_Session.Reset(); };
    network.OnPacket = [](const void* data, std::size_t size) {
        if (!data || size < 4) return false;
        auto p = static_cast<const unsigned char*>(data);
        if (p[0] != stp::PacketId || p[1] != 'S' || p[2] != 'T' || p[3] != 'P') return false;
        stp::Frame frame;
        if (g_Active && stp::Decode(data, size, frame)) {
            bool wasReady = g_Session.ready;
            g_Session.Receive(frame, GetTickCount());
            if (!wasReady && g_Session.ready) LogStatus("Server accepted handshake; checks active");
        }
        return true;
    };
    for (auto worker : {IdentityWorker, ScanWorker, IntegrityWorker}) {
        auto h = CreateThread(nullptr, 0, worker, nullptr, 0, nullptr);
        if (!h) { OutputDebugStringA("STP Vanguard: failed to start worker\n"); return 0; }
        CloseHandle(h);
    }
    bool warned = false;
    while (true) {
        auto samp = GetModuleHandleA("samp.dll");
        if (samp && network.Initialize(samp)) { Sleep(2000); continue; }
        if (samp && !warned) { LogStatus("Waiting for SA-MP RakClient (supported R1/R3/R5)"); warned = true; }
        Sleep(250);
    }
}

BOOL APIENTRY DllMain(HMODULE module, DWORD reason, LPVOID) {
    if (reason == DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH) {
        g_Self = module;
        DisableThreadLibraryCalls(module);
        auto thread = CreateThread(nullptr, 0, Bootstrap, nullptr, 0, nullptr);
        if (!thread) return FALSE;
        CloseHandle(thread);
    }
    return TRUE;
}

```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/client/network.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once
#include <Windows.h>
#include <atomic>
#include <functional>
#include <mutex>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>
#include <types.hpp>
#include "raknet/rakclient.h"

// Attach to the existing SA-MP RakClient, including a sampvoice wrapper if present.
// No LoadLibrary(samp.dll), factory detour, or dependency on ASI load order.
class Network {
public:
    static Network& Instance() { static Network n; return n; }
    std::function<void()> OnTick;
    std::function<bool(const void*, std::size_t)> OnPacket;
    std::function<bool(const void*, std::size_t)> QueueReport;
    std::function<void()> OnDisconnect;

    static bool Readable(const void* p, std::size_t n) {
        MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION m{};
        if (!p || !VirtualQuery(p, &m, sizeof(m)) || m.State != MEM_COMMIT || (m.Protect & (PAGE_NOACCESS | PAGE_GUARD))) return false;
        auto begin = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(p);
        auto end = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(m.BaseAddress) + m.RegionSize;
        return begin <= end && n <= end - begin;
    }
    static bool Executable(const void* p) {
        MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION m{};
        return p && VirtualQuery(p, &m, sizeof(m)) && m.State == MEM_COMMIT &&
            !(m.Protect & (PAGE_GUARD | PAGE_NOACCESS)) &&
            (m.Protect & (PAGE_EXECUTE | PAGE_EXECUTE_READ | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY));
    }
    bool Initialize(HMODULE samp) {
        if (!samp) return false;
        auto base = reinterpret_cast<std::uint8_t*>(samp);
        auto dos = reinterpret_cast<IMAGE_DOS_HEADER*>(base);
        auto pe = reinterpret_cast<IMAGE_NT_HEADERS*>(base + dos->e_lfanew);
        std::uint32_t netOffset = 0, rakOffset = 0;
        switch (pe->OptionalHeader.AddressOfEntryPoint) {
        case 0x31DF13: netOffset = 0x21A0F8; rakOffset = 0x3C9; break;
        case 0xCC4D0: netOffset = 0x26E8DC; rakOffset = 0x2C; break;
        case 0xCBC90: netOffset = 0x26EB94; rakOffset = 0; break;
        default: return false; // Unknown versions require a verified profile.
        }
        if (!Readable(base + netOffset, sizeof(void*))) return false;
        auto game = *reinterpret_cast<std::uint8_t**>(base + netOffset);
        if (!Readable(game, rakOffset + sizeof(void*))) return false;
        auto client = *reinterpret_cast<RakClientInterface**>(game + rakOffset);
        if (!Readable(client, sizeof(void*))) return false;
        auto table = *reinterpret_cast<void***>(client);
        if (!Readable(table, 10 * sizeof(void*))) return false;
        if (!Executable(table[7]) || !Executable(table[8]) || !Executable(table[9])) return false;
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        if (table[8] == reinterpret_cast<void*>(ReceiveHook)) { client_.store(client); return true; }
        // Never overwrite another hook after installing ours: it may chain to us.
        if (installed_) return false;
        DWORD old;
        if (!VirtualProtect(table + 8, sizeof(void*), PAGE_READWRITE, &old)) return false;
        receive_ = reinterpret_cast<ReceiveFn>(table[8]);
        client_.store(client);
        InterlockedExchangePointer(table + 8, reinterpret_cast<void*>(ReceiveHook));
        VirtualProtect(table + 8, sizeof(void*), old, &old);
        installed_ = true;
        return true;
    }
    RakClientInterface* GetRakClient() const { return client_.load(); }
    bool SendPacket(const void* data, std::size_t size) {
        return QueueReport && QueueReport(data, size); // workers enqueue; only game thread calls RakNet
    }
    bool SendRaw(const std::vector<std::uint8_t>& data) {
        auto client = GetRakClient();
        if (!client || data.empty() || !client->IsConnected()) return false;
        BitStream stream(const_cast<unsigned char*>(data.data()), static_cast<unsigned>(data.size()), false);
        return client->Send(&stream, HIGH_PRIORITY, RELIABLE_ORDERED, 7);
    }
private:
    using ReceiveFn = Packet* (__thiscall*)(RakClientInterface*);
    static Packet* __fastcall ReceiveHook(RakClientInterface* self, void*) {
        auto& n = Instance();
        // Limit consumed packets per frame, leaving normal GTA packets untouched.
        if (n.OnTick) n.OnTick();
        for (unsigned i = 0; i < 32; ++i) {
            auto packet = n.receive_(self);
            if (packet && packet->data && packet->length && (packet->data[0] == 21 || packet->data[0] == 22)) {
                if (n.OnDisconnect) n.OnDisconnect();
            }
            if (!packet || !n.OnPacket || !n.OnPacket(packet->data, packet->length)) return packet;
            self->DeallocatePacket(packet);
        }
        return nullptr;
    }
    std::atomic<RakClientInterface*> client_{nullptr};
    ReceiveFn receive_ = nullptr;
    bool installed_ = false;
    std::mutex mutex_;
};

```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/client/obfuscate.h`:

```h
/* --------------------------------- ABOUT -------------------------------------

Original Author: Adam Yaxley
Website: https://github.com/adamyaxley
License: See end of file

Obfuscate
Guaranteed compile-time string literal obfuscation library for C++14

Usage:
Pass string literals into the AY_OBFUSCATE macro to obfuscate them at compile
time. AY_OBFUSCATE returns a reference to an ay::obfuscated_data object with the
following traits:
	- Guaranteed obfuscation of string
	The passed string is encrypted with a simple XOR cipher at compile-time to
	prevent it being viewable in the binary image
	- Global lifetime
	The actual instantiation of the ay::obfuscated_data takes place inside a
	lambda as a function level static
	- Implicitly convertible to a char*
	This means that you can pass it directly into functions that would normally
	take a char* or a const char*

Example:
const char* obfuscated_string = AY_OBFUSCATE("Hello World");
std::cout << obfuscated_string << std::endl;

----------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

#pragma once
#if __cplusplus >= 202002L
#define AY_CONSTEVAL consteval
#else
#define AY_CONSTEVAL constexpr
#endif

// Workaround for __LINE__ not being constexpr when /ZI (Edit and Continue) is enabled in Visual Studio
// See: https://developercommunity.visualstudio.com/t/-line-cannot-be-used-as-an-argument-for-constexpr/195665
#ifdef _MSC_VER
#define AY_CAT(X,Y) AY_CAT2(X,Y)
#define AY_CAT2(X,Y) X##Y
#define AY_LINE int(AY_CAT(__LINE__,U))
#else
#define AY_LINE __LINE__
#endif

#ifndef AY_OBFUSCATE_DEFAULT_KEY
	// The default 64 bit key to obfuscate strings with.
	// This can be user specified by defining AY_OBFUSCATE_DEFAULT_KEY before 
	// including obfuscate.h
#define AY_OBFUSCATE_DEFAULT_KEY ay::generate_key(AY_LINE)
#endif

namespace ay
{
	using size_type = unsigned long long;
	using key_type = unsigned long long;

	// libstdc++ has std::remove_cvref_t<T> since C++20, but because not every user will be 
	// able or willing to link to the STL, we prefer to do this functionality ourselves here.
	template <typename T>
	struct remove_const_ref {
		using type = T;
	};

	template <typename T>
	struct remove_const_ref<T&> {
		using type = T;
	};

	template <typename T>
	struct remove_const_ref<const T> {
		using type = T;
	};

	template <typename T>
	struct remove_const_ref<const T&> {
		using type = T;
	};

	template <typename T>
	using char_type = typename remove_const_ref<T>::type;

	// Generate a pseudo-random key that spans all 8 bytes
	AY_CONSTEVAL key_type generate_key(key_type seed)
	{
		// Use the MurmurHash3 64-bit finalizer to hash our seed
		key_type key = seed;
		key ^= (key >> 33);
		key *= 0xff51afd7ed558ccd;
		key ^= (key >> 33);
		key *= 0xc4ceb9fe1a85ec53;
		key ^= (key >> 33);

		// Make sure that a bit in each byte is set
		key |= 0x0101010101010101ull;

		return key;
	}

	// Obfuscates or deobfuscates data with key
	template <typename CHAR_TYPE>
	constexpr void cipher(CHAR_TYPE* data, size_type size, key_type key)
	{
		// Obfuscate with a simple XOR cipher based on key
		for (size_type i = 0; i < size; i++)
		{
			data[i] ^= CHAR_TYPE((key >> ((i % 8) * 8)) & 0xFF);
		}
	}

	// Obfuscates a string at compile time
	template <size_type N, key_type KEY, typename CHAR_TYPE = char>
	class obfuscator
	{
	public:
		// Obfuscates the string 'data' on construction
		AY_CONSTEVAL obfuscator(const CHAR_TYPE* data)
		{
			// Copy data
			for (size_type i = 0; i < N; i++)
			{
				m_data[i] = data[i];
			}

			// On construction each of the characters in the string is
			// obfuscated with an XOR cipher based on key
			cipher(m_data, N, KEY);
		}

		constexpr const CHAR_TYPE* data() const
		{
			return &m_data[0];
		}

		AY_CONSTEVAL size_type size() const
		{
			return N;
		}

		AY_CONSTEVAL key_type key() const
		{
			return KEY;
		}

	private:

		CHAR_TYPE m_data[N]{};
	};

	// Handles decryption and re-encryption of an encrypted string at runtime
	template <size_type N, key_type KEY, typename CHAR_TYPE = char>
	class obfuscated_data
	{
	public:
		obfuscated_data(const obfuscator<N, KEY, CHAR_TYPE>& obfuscator)
		{
			// Copy obfuscated data
			for (size_type i = 0; i < N; i++)
			{
				m_data[i] = obfuscator.data()[i];
			}
		}

		~obfuscated_data()
		{
			// Zero m_data to remove it from memory
			for (size_type i = 0; i < N; i++)
			{
				m_data[i] = 0;
			}
		}

		// Returns a pointer to the plain text string, decrypting it if
		// necessary
		operator CHAR_TYPE* ()
		{
			decrypt();
			return m_data;
		}

		// Manually decrypt the string
		void decrypt()
		{
			if (m_encrypted)
			{
				cipher(m_data, N, KEY);
				m_encrypted = false;
			}
		}

		// Manually re-encrypt the string
		void encrypt()
		{
			if (!m_encrypted)
			{
				cipher(m_data, N, KEY);
				m_encrypted = true;
			}
		}

		// Returns true if this string is currently encrypted, false otherwise.
		bool is_encrypted() const
		{
			return m_encrypted;
		}

	private:

		// Local storage for the string. Call is_encrypted() to check whether or
		// not the string is currently obfuscated.
		CHAR_TYPE m_data[N];

		// Whether data is currently encrypted
		bool m_encrypted{ true };
	};

	// This function exists purely to extract the number of elements 'N' in the
	// array 'data'
	template <size_type N, key_type KEY = AY_OBFUSCATE_DEFAULT_KEY, typename CHAR_TYPE = char>
	AY_CONSTEVAL auto make_obfuscator(const CHAR_TYPE(&data)[N])
	{
		return obfuscator<N, KEY, CHAR_TYPE>(data);
	}
}

// Obfuscates the string 'data' at compile-time and returns a reference to a
// ay::obfuscated_data object with global lifetime that has functions for
// decrypting the string and is also implicitly convertable to a char*
#define AY_OBFUSCATE(data) AY_OBFUSCATE_KEY(data, AY_OBFUSCATE_DEFAULT_KEY)

// Obfuscates the string 'data' with 'key' at compile-time and returns a
// reference to a ay::obfuscated_data object with global lifetime that has
// functions for decrypting the string and is also implicitly convertable to a
// char*
#define AY_OBFUSCATE_KEY(data, key) \
	[]() -> ay::obfuscated_data<sizeof(data)/sizeof(data[0]), key, ay::char_type<decltype(*data)>>& { \
		static_assert(sizeof(decltype(key)) == sizeof(ay::key_type), "key must be a 64 bit unsigned integer"); \
		static_assert((key) >= (1ull << 56), "key must span all 8 bytes"); \
		using char_type = ay::char_type<decltype(*data)>; \
		constexpr auto n = sizeof(data)/sizeof(data[0]); \
		constexpr auto obfuscator = ay::make_obfuscator<n, key, char_type>(data); \
		thread_local auto obfuscated_data = ay::obfuscated_data<n, key, char_type>(obfuscator); \
		return obfuscated_data; \
	}()

/* -------------------------------- LICENSE ------------------------------------

Public Domain (http://www.unlicense.org)

This is free and unencumbered software released into the public domain.

Anyone is free to copy, modify, publish, use, compile, sell, or distribute this
software, either in source code form or as a compiled binary, for any purpose,
commercial or non-commercial, and by any means.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------- */#pragma once
```
