```cpp
        SecureEntry entry;
        entry.id = raw.id;
        entry.key = (rand() << 16) | rand();
        entry.salt = (rand() << 16) | rand();

        std::string plain_hex = std::string(raw.hex); // AY_OBFUSCATE จะถูกถอดตอน runtime

        ubyte_t plain_hash[16];
        HexToBytes(plain_hex.c_str(), plain_hash);

        DistortHash(plain_hash, entry.enc_hash, entry.key, entry.salt);
        g_SecureDatabase.push_back(entry);

        // Wipe ทิ้ง
        volatile char* wipe = (volatile char*)plain_hex.data();
        for (size_t i = 0; i < plain_hex.size(); ++i) wipe[i] = 0;
    }
    {
        SecureEntry entry{}; entry.id = 221; entry.key = 0x294AB; entry.salt = 0x123;
        ubyte_t hash[16]; HexToBytes("E213D5630486F2BE79952AAAB3DE4445", hash);
        DistortHash(hash, entry.enc_hash, entry.key, entry.salt); g_SecureDatabase.push_back(entry);
    }
    raw_cheats.clear();
}


static void PerformExternalCheatScan()
{
    HANDLE hSnapshot = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0);
    if (hSnapshot == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;

    PROCESSENTRY32 pe32 = { sizeof(pe32) };
    if (!Process32First(hSnapshot, &pe32))
    {
        CloseHandle(hSnapshot);
        return;
    }

    do
    {
        if (!g_Active) break;
        g_Session.progress.fetch_add(1);
        // ข้าม process ระดับระบบ / เล็กน้อย
        if (pe32.th32ProcessID <= 100) continue;

        std::string pName = pe32.szExeFile;
        std::transform(pName.begin(), pName.end(), pName.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });

        if (pe32.th32ProcessID == GetCurrentProcessId()) continue;

        char exePath[MAX_PATH] = { 0 };
        HANDLE hProc = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, FALSE, pe32.th32ProcessID);
        if (!hProc) continue;

        DWORD size = MAX_PATH;
        if (!QueryFullProcessImageNameA(hProc, 0, exePath, &size))
        {
            CloseHandle(hProc);
            continue;
        }

        // ตรวจขนาดไฟล์ก่อน (ลดภาระ)
        LARGE_INTEGER fileSize{};
        HANDLE hFile = CreateFileA(exePath, GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, NULL, OPEN_EXISTING, 0, NULL);
        if (hFile == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE)
        {
            CloseHandle(hProc);
            continue;
        }
        const bool sizeOK = GetFileSizeEx(hFile, &fileSize) != FALSE;
        CloseHandle(hFile);

        if (!sizeOK || fileSize.QuadPart <= 0 || fileSize.QuadPart > 25 * 1024 * 1024) // 25MB
        {
            CloseHandle(hProc);
            continue;
        }

        // คำนวณ MD5
        auto fileMd5 = GetFileMD5Bytes(exePath);
        if (fileMd5.size() != 16)
        {
            CloseHandle(hProc);
            continue;
        }

        // แปลง MD5 เป็น Hex String (ใช้ร่วมกันทั้ง 2 ส่วน)
        char computedHex[33] = { 0 };
        for (int b = 0; b < 16; ++b)
            sprintf(computedHex + b * 2, "%02X", fileMd5[b]);

        // === [ส่วนเพิ่มใหม่] ส่ง MD5 โปรแกรมภายนอกให้ Server ตรวจสอบแบบ Dynamic ===
        {
            std::string hashStr = computedHex;
            bool shouldReport = false;

            {
                std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_DynamicHashMutex);
                if (g_ReportedDynamicHashes.find(hashStr) == g_ReportedDynamicHashes.end())
                {
                    g_ReportedDynamicHashes.insert(hashStr);
                    shouldReport = true;
                }
            }

            if (shouldReport)
            {
                std::string dynFilename = pName;

                ubyte_t packet[256] = { 0 };
                packet[0] = kPacketCheatDetected;
                packet[1] = 205;        // ID สำหรับ Dynamic File Report
                packet[2] = 2;          // Type 2 = Process (โปรแกรมภายนอก)

                memcpy(packet + 3, fileMd5.data(), 16);

                ubyte_t nameLen = static_cast<ubyte_t>(std::min(dynFilename.length(), size_t(200)));
                packet[19] = nameLen;
                memcpy(packet + 20, dynFilename.c_str(), nameLen);

                if (!Network::Instance().SendPacket(packet, 20 + nameLen)) {
                    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_DynamicHashMutex);
                    g_ReportedDynamicHashes.erase(hashStr);
                }
            }
        }
        // ========================================================

        // ตรวจสอบกับฐานข้อมูล cheat ที่รู้จัก
        for (size_t i = 0; kExternalCheats[i].md5Hash != nullptr; ++i)
        {
            if (_stricmp(computedHex, kExternalCheats[i].md5Hash) == 0)
            {
                ubyte_t packet[2] = { kPacketCheatDetected, kExternalCheats[i].cheatId };
                Network::Instance().SendPacket(packet, sizeof(packet));

                CloseHandle(hProc);
                CloseHandle(hSnapshot);
                return;                 // ออกจากฟังก์ชันทันที
            }
        }

        CloseHandle(hProc);

    } while (Process32Next(hSnapshot, &pe32));

    CloseHandle(hSnapshot);
}


// =============================================================
// ส่วนที่เหลือ (เหมือนเดิม + การเรียกใหม่)
// =============================================================
bool IsDebugged() {
    if (IsDebuggerPresent()) return true;
    BOOL isDbg = FALSE;
    CheckRemoteDebuggerPresent(GetCurrentProcess(), &isDbg);
    return (isDbg == TRUE);
}


static std::string GameDirectory() {
    char path[MAX_PATH]{};
    GetModuleFileNameA(nullptr, path, MAX_PATH);
    return fs::path(path).parent_path().string();
}
static bool IsWhitelistedModule(const std::string& path) {
    char windows[MAX_PATH]{};
    GetWindowsDirectoryA(windows, MAX_PATH);
    std::string prefix = windows;
    std::transform(prefix.begin(), prefix.end(), prefix.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });
    prefix += '\\';
    if (path.compare(0, prefix.size(), prefix) == 0) return true;
    // Preserve common recording / overlay compatibility with directory boundaries.
    static const char* components[]{"\\nvidia corporation\\", "\\discord\\", "\\steam\\",
        "\\obs-studio\\", "\\rivatuner statistics server\\", "\\bandicam\\", "\\windhawk\\",
        "\\apple application support\\", "\\overwolf\\", "\\nahimic\\", "\\a-volute\\"};
    for (auto component : components) if (path.find(component) != std::string::npos) return true;
    return false;
}

static void PerformModuleScan()
{
    HANDLE hProcess = GetCurrentProcess();
    HMODULE hMods[1024];
    DWORD cbNeeded;
    if (!EnumProcessModules(hProcess, hMods, sizeof(hMods), &cbNeeded)) return;

    int moduleCount = (std::min)(cbNeeded / sizeof(HMODULE), DWORD(sizeof(hMods) / sizeof(hMods[0])));

    // ดึง game directory ปัจจุบัน (ทำให้ match แน่นอน)
    char gameDir[MAX_PATH] = { 0 };
    strncpy_s(gameDir, GameDirectory().c_str(), _TRUNCATE);
    std::string gameDirLower = gameDir;
    std::transform(gameDirLower.begin(), gameDirLower.end(), gameDirLower.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });
    if (!gameDirLower.empty() && gameDirLower.back() != '\\') {
        gameDirLower += '\\';
    }

    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_ReportedMutex);

    for (int i = 0; i < moduleCount; ++i)
    {
        char szModPath[MAX_PATH] = { 0 };
        if (GetModuleFileNameA(hMods[i], szModPath, sizeof(szModPath)) == 0) continue;

        std::string modPath = szModPath;
        std::string modPathLower = modPath;
        std::transform(modPathLower.begin(), modPathLower.end(), modPathLower.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });

        // ==================== แก้ false positive ====================
        // 1. ถ้าอยู่ในโฟลเดอร์เกม (รวม modloader, .data, plugins ฯลฯ) → ข้ามทันที
        if (modPathLower.find(gameDirLower) == 0) continue;

        // 2. ถ้าเป็น whitelist (Windhawk, Apple CoreAudio, Windows system ฯลฯ) → ข้าม
        if (IsWhitelistedModule(modPathLower)) continue;

        // 3. ตรวจเฉพาะ .asi และ .dll เท่านั้น
        std::string ext = fs::path(modPath).extension().string();
        std::transform(ext.begin(), ext.end(), ext.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });
        if (ext != ".asi" && ext != ".dll") continue;

        // ป้องกันสแปมรายงานซ้ำ
        if (g_ReportedInjectedModules.find(modPathLower) != g_ReportedInjectedModules.end()) {
            continue;
        }

        // บันทึกว่าส่งรายงานแล้ว
        g_ReportedInjectedModules.insert(modPathLower);

        // ส่งแจ้งเตือนไป Server (code 200 = Injected Module)
        ubyte_t packet[256] = { 0 };
        packet[0] = kPacketCheatDetected;
        packet[1] = 200;
        ubyte_t len = static_cast<ubyte_t>(std::min(modPath.length(), size_t(253)));
        packet[2] = len;
        memcpy(packet + 3, modPath.c_str(), len);

        // ส่ง 5 ครั้ง (พอแล้ว ไม่ต้องเยอะเกิน)
        Network::Instance().SendPacket(packet, 3 + len);
    }
}

static void PerformSecureCheatScan()
{
    if (IsDebugged())
    {
        ubyte_t buf[2] = { kPacketCheatDetected, 254 };
        Network::Instance().SendPacket(buf, sizeof(buf));
    }

    try
    {
        for (const auto& entry : fs::recursive_directory_iterator(GameDirectory(), fs::directory_options::skip_permission_denied))
        {
            g_Session.progress.fetch_add(1);
            if (!g_Active) return;
            Sleep(1);
            if (entry.is_symlink() || !entry.is_regular_file()) continue;

            // ข้ามไฟล์ที่ใหญ่เกินไป (ป้องกัน lag)
            if (entry.file_size() > 50 * 1024 * 1024) continue;

            std::string path = entry.path().string();
            std::string ext = entry.path().extension().string();
            std::transform(ext.begin(), ext.end(), ext.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });

            // ตรวจเฉพาะนามสกุลที่เกี่ยวข้องกับ mod/cheat/script
            if (ext != ".asi" && ext != ".dll" && ext != ".cs" && ext != ".sf" &&
                ext != ".cleo" && ext != ".lua" && ext != ".luac" &&
                ext != ".cfg" && ext != ".dat" && ext != ".ini" && ext != ".xml")
                continue;

            // ชื่อไฟล์แบบ lowercase เพื่อตรวจสอบ
            std::string filename_lower = entry.path().filename().string();
            std::transform(filename_lower.begin(), filename_lower.end(), filename_lower.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });

            // ==================== ตรวจไฟล์ต้องห้าม (Forbidden Files) ====================
            const char* forbiddenName = nullptr;
            ubyte_t forbiddenID = 0;

            if (filename_lower == "moonloader.asi") {
                forbiddenName = "MoonLoader.asi";
                forbiddenID = 200;
            }
            else if (filename_lower == "lua51.dll") {
                forbiddenName = "lua51.dll";
                forbiddenID = 200;
            }

            if (kClientType == 1)  // SA-MP
            {
                if (filename_lower == "cleo.asi") {
                    forbiddenName = "CLEO.asi";
                    forbiddenID = 40;
                }
                else if (filename_lower == "sampfuncs.asi") {
                    forbiddenName = "SAMPFUNCS.asi";
                    forbiddenID = 41;
                }
            }

            if (kClientType == 1 && (ext == ".cs" || ext == ".cleo" || ext == ".sf")) {
                forbiddenName = "CLEO script"; forbiddenID = 40;
            }
            if (forbiddenName != nullptr)
            {
                // 1. ส่งแพ็กเก็ตแจ้งเตือนเซิร์ฟเวอร์
                ubyte_t buf[2] = { kPacketCheatDetected, forbiddenID };
                Network::Instance().SendPacket(buf, sizeof(buf));

                return;
            }

            // ==================== คำนวณ MD5 ====================
            auto fileHash = GetFileMD5Bytes(path);
            if (fileHash.size() != 16) continue;

            // ==================== ตรวจสอบกับฐานข้อมูล Secure (Static Detection) ====================
            for (const auto& dbEntry : g_SecureDatabase)
            {
                if (stp::AllowCleoSignature(kClientType, dbEntry.id)) continue;
                ubyte_t candidate[16];
                DistortHash(fileHash.data(), candidate, dbEntry.key, dbEntry.salt);

                if (memcmp(candidate, dbEntry.enc_hash, 16) == 0)
                {
                    ubyte_t buf[2] = { kPacketCheatDetected, static_cast<ubyte_t>(dbEntry.id) };
                    Network::Instance().SendPacket(buf, sizeof(buf));
                    return;   // พบ cheat แบบ static → หยุดทันที
                }
            }

            // ==================== [Dynamic Report] ส่ง MD5 ทุกไฟล์ไปให้ Server ตรวจสอบ ====================
            {
                char hashHex[33] = { 0 };
                for (int b = 0; b < 16; ++b) {
                    sprintf(hashHex + b * 2, "%02X", fileHash[b]);
                }
                std::string hashStr = hashHex;

                bool shouldReport = false;
                {
                    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_DynamicHashMutex);
                    if (g_ReportedDynamicHashes.find(hashStr) == g_ReportedDynamicHashes.end()) {
                        g_ReportedDynamicHashes.insert(hashStr);
                        shouldReport = true;
                    }
                }

                if (shouldReport)
                {
                    std::string dynFilename = entry.path().filename().string();

                    constexpr size_t MAX_NAME_LEN = 200;
                    ubyte_t nameLen = static_cast<ubyte_t>(std::min(dynFilename.length(), MAX_NAME_LEN));

                    ubyte_t packet[256] = { 0 };        // ศูนย์ทั้งหมดเพื่อความปลอดภัย
                    packet[0] = kPacketCheatDetected;
                    packet[1] = 205;                    // Dynamic File Report
                    packet[2] = 1;                      // Type 1 = โฟลเดอร์เกม

                    memcpy(packet + 3, fileHash.data(), 16);
                    packet[19] = nameLen;
                    memcpy(packet + 20, dynFilename.c_str(), nameLen);

                    if (!Network::Instance().SendPacket(packet, 20 + nameLen)) {
                    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_DynamicHashMutex);
                    g_ReportedDynamicHashes.erase(hashStr);
                }
                }
            }
            // ========================================================
        }
    }
    catch (...)
    {
        // เงียบไว้เหมือนเดิม (ป้องกัน crash จาก permission หรือ symlink)
    }
}

// ช่วยแปลง path เป็นชื่อไฟล์ lowercase
static std::string GetModuleNameLower(const char* fullPath) {
    if (!fullPath || !*fullPath) return "";
    std::string path = fullPath;
    std::transform(path.begin(), path.end(), path.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });
    size_t lastSlash = path.find_last_of("\\/");
    if (lastSlash != std::string::npos) {
        path = path.substr(lastSlash + 1);
    }
    return path;
}

// เก็บรายชื่อ module ทั้งหมดตอนเริ่มต้น (เรียกครั้งเดียว)
static void CaptureInitialModuleList() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_ModuleMutex);

    HMODULE hMods[2048];  // เพิ่มขนาด buffer เผื่อ module เยอะ
    DWORD cbNeeded;
    if (!EnumProcessModules(GetCurrentProcess(), hMods, sizeof(hMods), &cbNeeded))
        return;

    DWORD count = (std::min)(cbNeeded / sizeof(HMODULE), DWORD(sizeof(hMods) / sizeof(hMods[0])));

    g_InitialModules.clear();

    for (DWORD i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        char szPath[MAX_PATH] = { 0 };
        if (GetModuleFileNameA(hMods[i], szPath, MAX_PATH)) {
            std::string name = szPath;
            std::transform(name.begin(), name.end(), name.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });
            if (!name.empty()) {
                g_InitialModules.insert(name);
            }
        }
    }

    g_FirstModuleScanDone = true;
}

static bool DetectNewInjectedModules() {
    if (!g_FirstModuleScanDone) return false;

    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_ModuleMutex);

    HMODULE hMods[2048];
    DWORD cbNeeded;
    if (!EnumProcessModules(GetCurrentProcess(), hMods, sizeof(hMods), &cbNeeded))
        return false;

    DWORD count = (std::min)(cbNeeded / sizeof(HMODULE), DWORD(sizeof(hMods) / sizeof(hMods[0])));

    // ดึง game directory ปัจจุบัน
    char gameDir[MAX_PATH] = { 0 };
    strncpy_s(gameDir, GameDirectory().c_str(), _TRUNCATE);

    std::string gameDirLower = gameDir;
    std::transform(gameDirLower.begin(), gameDirLower.end(), gameDirLower.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });
    if (!gameDirLower.empty() && gameDirLower.back() != '\\') {
        gameDirLower += '\\';
    }

    for (DWORD i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        char szPath[MAX_PATH] = { 0 };
        if (GetModuleFileNameA(hMods[i], szPath, MAX_PATH) == 0) continue;

        std::string modPathLower = szPath;
        std::transform(modPathLower.begin(), modPathLower.end(), modPathLower.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });

        std::string nameLower = GetModuleNameLower(szPath);
        if (nameLower.empty()) continue;

        // === เงื่อนไขใหม่ (แก้ false positive) ===
        // 1. ถ้าอยู่ในโฟลเดอร์เกม → ข้ามเลย (เป็น mod ปกติ)
        if (modPathLower.find(gameDirLower) == 0) continue;

        // 2. ถ้าเคยมีตั้งแต่แรก → ข้าม
        if (g_InitialModules.find(modPathLower) != g_InitialModules.end()) continue;

        // 3. ถ้าอยู่ใน whitelist (Windhawk, Apple, etc.) → ข้าม
        if (IsWhitelistedModule(modPathLower)) continue;

        // === ถ้าผ่านทุกเงื่อนไข → เป็น injection จริง ===
        char msg[512];
        snprintf(msg, sizeof(msg),
            AY_OBFUSCATE("Suspicious injected module detected!\n"
                "Module: %s\n"
                "Path: %s\n\n"
                "This is NOT a normal game file."),
            nameLower.c_str(), szPath);

        ubyte_t packet[256] = { kPacketCheatDetected, 210 };
        size_t len = std::min(strlen(msg), size_t(200u));
        packet[2] = static_cast<ubyte_t>(len);
        memcpy(packet + 3, msg, len);

        Network::Instance().SendPacket(packet, 3 + len);

        g_InitialModules.insert(modPathLower);
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}



static void CheckGameFlags() {
    struct Flag { std::uintptr_t address; ubyte_t code; };
    static const Flag flags[]{{0xB7CEE4, 106}, {0x96916C, 112}};
    for (const auto& flag : flags) {
        auto p = reinterpret_cast<volatile BYTE*>(flag.address);
        if (Network::Readable(const_cast<const BYTE*>(p), 1) && *p == 1) {
            const ubyte_t report[]{251, flag.code};
            Network::Instance().SendPacket(report, sizeof(report));
            // Existing GTA SA 1.0 US policy: reset these cheat flags only.
            *p = 0;
        }
    }
}

static void CheckJoypad() {
    const UINT devices = joyGetNumDevs();
    for (UINT i = 0; i < devices; ++i) {
        JOYINFOEX info{}; info.dwSize = sizeof(info); info.dwFlags = JOY_RETURNALL;
        if (joyGetPosEx(i, &info) == JOYERR_NOERROR &&
            (info.dwButtons || abs(int(info.dwXpos) - 32767) > 6000 || abs(int(info.dwYpos) - 32767) > 6000)) joyReleaseCapture(i);
    }
}

DWORD WINAPI ScanWorker(LPVOID) {
    SetThreadPriority(GetCurrentThread(), THREAD_PRIORITY_BELOW_NORMAL);
    InitializeSecureDB();
    CaptureInitialModuleList();
    DWORD files = 0, modules = 0, external = 0, flags = 0, joypad = 0;
    bool initial = true;
    auto generation = g_Session.generation.load();
    while (true) {
        g_Session.progress.fetch_add(1);
        if (generation != g_Session.generation.load()) {
            generation = g_Session.generation.load();
            { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_DynamicHashMutex); g_ReportedDynamicHashes.clear(); }
            { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_ReportedMutex); g_ReportedInjectedModules.clear(); }
            initial = true;
        }
        if (g_Active && g_Session.ready) {
            const DWORD now = GetTickCount();
            try {
                if (initial || now - files >= 45000) { PerformSecureCheatScan(); files = GetTickCount(); initial = false; }
                if (now - modules >= 7000) { PerformModuleScan(); DetectNewInjectedModules(); modules = GetTickCount(); }
                if (now - external >= 9000) { PerformExternalCheatScan(); external = GetTickCount(); }
                if (now - flags >= 1800) { CheckGameFlags(); flags = now; }
                if (now - joypad >= 3500) { CheckJoypad(); joypad = now; }
            } catch (...) { OutputDebugStringA("STP Vanguard: scan pass failed; retrying\n"); }
        } else { initial = true; }
        Sleep(250);
    }
}

DWORD WINAPI IntegrityWorker(LPVOID) {
    SetThreadPriority(GetCurrentThread(), THREAD_PRIORITY_BELOW_NORMAL);
    CodeIntegrity integrity;
    g_Session.integrity = integrity.Initialize(g_Self);
    while (true) {
        if (!integrity.Check()) {
            g_Session.integrity = false;
            const ubyte_t report[]{251, 222};
            Network::Instance().SendPacket(report, sizeof(report));
        }
        Sleep(250);
    }
}
```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/client/whirlpool.h`:

```h
/* whirlpool.h */
#ifndef WHIRLPOOL_H
#define WHIRLPOOL_H
#include "ustd.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#define whirlpool_block_size 64

	/* algorithm context */
	typedef struct whirlpool_ctx
	{
		uint64_t hash[8];    /* 512-bit algorithm internal hashing state */
		unsigned char message[whirlpool_block_size]; /* 512-bit buffer to hash */

		/* Note: original algorith uses 256-bit counter, allowing to hash up to
		   2^256 bits sized message. For optimization we use here 64-bit counter,
		   thus reducing maximal message size to 2^64 bits = 2 Exbibytes = 2^21 TiB) */
		uint64_t length;     /* number of processed bytes */
	} whirlpool_ctx;

	/* hash functions */

	void rhash_whirlpool_init(whirlpool_ctx* ctx);
	void rhash_whirlpool_update(whirlpool_ctx* ctx, const unsigned char* msg, size_t size);
	void rhash_whirlpool_final(whirlpool_ctx* ctx, unsigned char* result);

#ifdef __cplusplus
} /* extern "C" */
#endif /* __cplusplus */

#endif /* WHIRLPOOL_H */
```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/common/jump_hook.hpp`:

```hpp
/*
    This is a KeyListener project file
    Developer: CyberMor <cyber.mor.2020@gmail.com>

    See more here https://github.com/CyberMor/keylistener

    Copyright (c) Daniel (CyberMor) 2020 All rights reserved
*/

#pragma once

#include <cassert>

#include "types.hpp"
#include "patch.hpp"

struct JumpHook {

    JumpHook() noexcept = default;
    ~JumpHook() noexcept = default;
    JumpHook(const JumpHook&) = delete;
    JumpHook(JumpHook&&) noexcept = default;
    JumpHook& operator=(const JumpHook&) = delete;
    JumpHook& operator=(JumpHook&&) noexcept = default;

private:

#pragma pack(push, 1)

    struct JumpInstruction {

        JumpInstruction() = delete;
        ~JumpInstruction() noexcept = default;
        JumpInstruction(const JumpInstruction&) noexcept = default;
        JumpInstruction(JumpInstruction&&) noexcept = default;
        JumpInstruction& operator=(const JumpInstruction&) noexcept = default;
        JumpInstruction& operator=(JumpInstruction&&) noexcept = default;

    public:

        JumpInstruction(const sdword_t offset) noexcept
            : offset { offset }
        {}

    public:

         ubyte_t opcode = 0xE9;
        sdword_t offset;

    };

#pragma pack(pop)

public:

    JumpHook(const ptr_t inject, const cptr_t hook, const bool enabled = true) noexcept
        : _patch { inject, &JumpInstruction((sdword_t)(hook) - ((sdword_t)(inject) +
            (sdword_t)(sizeof(JumpInstruction)))), enabled }
    {
        assert(hook != nullptr);
    }

public:

    bool Valid() const noexcept
    {
        return _patch.Valid();
    }

    bool Invalid() const noexcept
    {
        return _patch.Invalid();
    }

public:

    bool Initialize(const ptr_t inject, const cptr_t hook, const bool enabled = true) noexcept
    {
        assert(hook != nullptr);

        return _patch.Initialize(inject, &JumpInstruction((sdword_t)(hook) -
            ((sdword_t)(inject) + (sdword_t)(sizeof(JumpInstruction)))), enabled);
    }

    void Deinitialize() noexcept
    {
        _patch.Deinitialize();
    }

public:

    void Enable() noexcept
    {
        _patch.Enable();
    }

    void Disable() noexcept
    {
        _patch.Disable();
    }

public:

    ptr_t Address() const noexcept
    {
        return _patch.Address();
    }

    constexpr size_t Length() const noexcept
    {
        return _patch.Length();
    }

private:

    Patch<sizeof(JumpInstruction)> _patch;

};
```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/common/patch.hpp`:

```hpp
/*
    This is a KeyListener project file
    Developer: CyberMor <cyber.mor.2020@gmail.com>

    See more here https://github.com/CyberMor/keylistener

    Copyright (c) Daniel (CyberMor) 2020 All rights reserved
*/

#pragma once

#include <cassert>
#include <cstring>
#include <utility>

#include "types.hpp"
#include "unprotect_scope.hpp"

template <size_t Size>
struct Patch {

    Patch() noexcept = default;
    ~Patch() noexcept
    {
        Disable();
    }

    Patch(const Patch&) = delete;
    Patch(Patch&& object) noexcept
        : _enabled { std::move(object._enabled) }
        , _upscope { std::move(object._upscope) }
    {
        std::memcpy(_patch_bytes, object._patch_bytes, Size);
        std::memcpy(_orig_bytes, object._orig_bytes, Size);

        object._enabled = false;
    }

    Patch& operator=(const Patch&) = delete;
    Patch& operator=(Patch&& object) noexcept
    {
        if (&object != this)
        {
            Disable();

            _enabled = std::move(object._enabled);
            _upscope = std::move(object._upscope);

            std::memcpy(_patch_bytes, object._patch_bytes, Size);
            std::memcpy(_orig_bytes, object._orig_bytes, Size);

            object._enabled = false;
        }

        return *this;
    }

public:

    Patch(const ptr_t address, const cptr_t patch, const bool enabled = true) noexcept
        : _upscope { address, false }
    {
        assert(address != nullptr);
        assert(patch   != nullptr);

        std::memcpy(_patch_bytes, patch, Size);
        std::memcpy(_orig_bytes, address, Size);

        if (enabled) Enable();
    }

public:

    bool Valid() const noexcept
    {
        return _upscope.Valid();
    }

    bool Invalid() const noexcept
    {
        return _upscope.Invalid();
    }

public:

    bool Initialize(const ptr_t address, const cptr_t patch, const bool enabled = true) noexcept
    {
        Disable();

        _upscope.Deinitialize();

        assert(address != nullptr);
        assert(patch   != nullptr);

        std::memcpy(_patch_bytes, patch, Size);
        std::memcpy(_orig_bytes, address, Size);

        return _upscope.Initialize(address, false) &&
            (enabled == false || (Enable(), _enabled == true));
    }

    void Deinitialize() noexcept
    {
        Disable();

        _upscope.Deinitialize();
    }

public:

    void Enable() noexcept
    {
        if (_enabled == false)
        {
            _upscope.Enable();
            std::memcpy(_upscope.Address(), _patch_bytes, Size);
#ifdef _WIN32
            FlushInstructionCache(GetCurrentProcess(), _upscope.Address(), Size);
#endif
            _upscope.Disable();
            _enabled = true;
        }
    }

    void Disable() noexcept
    {
        if (_enabled == true)
        {
            _upscope.Enable();
            std::memcpy(_upscope.Address(), _orig_bytes, Size);
#ifdef _WIN32
            FlushInstructionCache(GetCurrentProcess(), _upscope.Address(), Size);
#endif
            _upscope.Disable();
            _enabled = false;
        }
    }

public:

    ptr_t Address() const noexcept
    {
        return _upscope.Address();
    }

    constexpr size_t Length() const noexcept
    {
        return _upscope.Length();
    }

private:

    bool _enabled = false;

    ubyte_t _patch_bytes[Size];
    ubyte_t  _orig_bytes[Size];

    UnprotectScope<Size> _upscope;

};

```

```hpp
#pragma once
namespace stp {
constexpr bool AllowCleoSignature(unsigned type, int signature) {
    return type == 0 && (signature == 40 || signature == 41);
}
}
```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/common/protocol.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace stp {
using Bytes = std::vector<std::uint8_t>;
constexpr std::uint8_t PacketId = 249;
constexpr std::size_t MaxPayload = 256;
constexpr std::uint32_t HeartbeatMs = 10000;
constexpr std::uint32_t TimeoutMs = 45000;
enum class Kind : std::uint8_t { Hello = 1, Challenge, Response, Accepted, Report, ReportAck };
inline std::uint32_t Elapsed(std::uint32_t now, std::uint32_t then) { return now - then; }
inline std::uint32_t RetryMs(unsigned attempt) { return 1000u << (attempt > 3 ? 3 : attempt); }
inline void Put32(Bytes& out, std::uint32_t n) {
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) out.push_back(static_cast<std::uint8_t>(n >> (i * 8)));
}
inline std::uint32_t Get32(const std::uint8_t* p) {
    return std::uint32_t(p[0]) | std::uint32_t(p[1]) << 8 | std::uint32_t(p[2]) << 16 | std::uint32_t(p[3]) << 24;
}
struct Frame { Kind kind{}; std::uint32_t session = 0, sequence = 0; Bytes payload; };
inline Bytes Encode(Kind kind, std::uint32_t session, std::uint32_t sequence, const Bytes& payload = {}) {
    if (payload.size() > MaxPayload) return {};
    Bytes out{PacketId, 'S', 'T', 'P', 1, static_cast<std::uint8_t>(kind)};
    Put32(out, session); Put32(out, sequence);
    out.push_back(static_cast<std::uint8_t>(payload.size()));
    out.push_back(static_cast<std::uint8_t>(payload.size() >> 8));
    out.insert(out.end(), payload.begin(), payload.end());
    return out;
}
inline bool Decode(const void* raw, std::size_t size, Frame& f) {
    if (!raw || size < 16 || size > 16 + MaxPayload) return false;
    auto p = static_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(raw);
    if (p[0] != PacketId || p[1] != 'S' || p[2] != 'T' || p[3] != 'P' || p[4] != 1 || p[5] < 1 || p[5] > 6) return false;
    const auto n = std::size_t(p[14]) | std::size_t(p[15]) << 8;
    if (n != size - 16) return false;
    f.kind = static_cast<Kind>(p[5]); f.session = Get32(p + 6); f.sequence = Get32(p + 10);
    f.payload.assign(p + 16, p + size); return true;
}
inline bool IsHwid(const std::string& s) {
    if (s.size() != 36) return false;
    for (std::size_t n = 0; n < s.size(); ++n) {
        unsigned char c = s[n];
        if (n == 8 || n == 13 || n == 18 || n == 23) { if (c != '-') return false; continue; }
        if (!((c >= '0' && c <= '9') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'F') || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f'))) return false;
    }
    return true;
}
struct Identity { std::uint8_t major = 9, minor = 11, patch = 0, type = 0; std::string hwid, hwid2; };
inline Bytes EncodeIdentity(const Identity& i) {
    if (!IsHwid(i.hwid) || !IsHwid(i.hwid2) || i.type > 1) return {};
    Bytes b{i.major, i.minor, i.patch, i.type, 36};
    b.insert(b.end(), i.hwid.begin(), i.hwid.end()); b.push_back(36);
    b.insert(b.end(), i.hwid2.begin(), i.hwid2.end()); return b;
}
inline bool DecodeIdentity(const Bytes& b, Identity& i) {
    if (b.size() != 78 || b[3] > 1 || b[4] != 36 || b[41] != 36) return false;
    i.major = b[0]; i.minor = b[1]; i.patch = b[2]; i.type = b[3];
    i.hwid.assign(b.begin() + 5, b.begin() + 41); i.hwid2.assign(b.begin() + 42, b.end());
    return IsHwid(i.hwid) && IsHwid(i.hwid2);
}
inline bool ValidReport(const Bytes& b) {
    if (b.size() < 2 || b[0] != 251) return false;
    if (b[1] == 205) return b.size() >= 21 && (b[2] == 1 || b[2] == 2) && b[19] > 0 && b[19] <= 200 && b.size() == 20u + b[19];
    if ((b[1] == 200 || b[1] == 210 || b[1] == 220) && b.size() != 2)
        return b.size() >= 4 && b[2] > 0 && b[2] <= 253 && b.size() == 3u + b[2];
    return b.size() == 2;
}
// Only one outstanding report is sent. Server callbacks run once even if ACKs are lost.
struct ReportSequence {
    std::uint32_t last = 0;
    bool Duplicate(std::uint32_t n) const { return n != 0 && n == last; }
    bool Next(std::uint32_t n) const { return n != 0 && n == last + 1; }
};
}
```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/common/scanner.hpp`:

```hpp
/*
    This is a KeyListener project file
    Developer: CyberMor <cyber.mor.2020@gmail.com>

    See more here https://github.com/CyberMor/keylistener

    Copyright (c) Daniel (CyberMor) 2020 All rights reserved
*/

#pragma once

#include <cassert>

#ifdef _WIN32
#include <Windows.h>
#else
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#endif

#include "types.hpp"

struct Scanner {

    Scanner() noexcept = default;
    ~Scanner() noexcept = default;
    Scanner(const Scanner&) noexcept = default;
    Scanner(Scanner&&) noexcept = default;
    Scanner& operator=(const Scanner&) noexcept = default;
    Scanner& operator=(Scanner&&) noexcept = default;

public:

    Scanner(const cptr_t address, const size_t size) noexcept
        : _address { address }
        , _size    { size }
    {}

public:

    bool Valid() const noexcept
    {
        return _address != nullptr && _size != 0;
    }

    bool Invalid() const noexcept
    {
        return _address == nullptr || _size == 0;
    }

public:

    bool Initialize(const cptr_t address, const size_t size) noexcept
    {
        return (_address = address) != nullptr &&
               (_size    = size)    != 0;
    }

    void Deinitialize() noexcept
    {
        _address = nullptr;
        _size    = 0;
    }

public:

    template <size_t Size>
    ptr_t Find(const char(&pattern)[Size], const char(&mask)[Size]) const noexcept
    {
        if (!_address || _size < Size - 1) return nullptr;

        const auto compare = [&](const char* const sequence) noexcept -> bool
        {
            for (size_t i = 0; i != Size - 1; ++i)
            {
                if (mask[i] == 'x' && pattern[i] != sequence[i])
                    return false;
            }

            return true;
        };

        const cstr_t border   = static_cast<cstr_t>(_address) + _size - (Size - 1);
              cstr_t iterator = static_cast<cstr_t>(_address);

        while (iterator <= border)
        {
            if (compare(iterator))
                return const_cast<str_t>(iterator);

            ++iterator;
        }

        return nullptr;
    }

private:

    cptr_t _address = nullptr;
    size_t _size    = 0;

};

inline std::pair<cptr_t, size_t> GetModuleInfo(const cptr_t address) noexcept
{
    std::pair<cptr_t, size_t> result;
#ifdef _WIN32
    if (MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION info; VirtualQuery(address, &info, sizeof(info)) != 0)
    {
        if (info.AllocationBase != nullptr)
        {
            const auto dos = static_cast<IMAGE_DOS_HEADER*>(info.AllocationBase);
            const auto pe = reinterpret_cast<IMAGE_NT_HEADERS*>(reinterpret_cast<size_t>(dos) + dos->e_lfanew);

            if (pe->Signature == IMAGE_NT_SIGNATURE)
            {
                if (pe->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage != 0)
                {
                    result.first = info.AllocationBase;
                    result.second = pe->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage;
                }
            }
        }
    }
#else
    if (Dl_info info; dladdr(address, &info) != 0)
    {
        if (struct stat buf; stat(info.dli_fname, &buf) == 0)
        {
            if (info.dli_fbase != nullptr && buf.st_size != 0)
            {
                result.first = info.dli_fbase;
                result.second = buf.st_size;
            }
        }
    }
#endif
    return result;
}
```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/common/session.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once
#include "protocol.hpp"
#include <array>
#include <atomic>
#include <deque>
#include <functional>
#include <mutex>
#include <algorithm>

namespace stp {
using Send = std::function<bool(const Bytes&)>;
using Nonce = std::array<std::uint8_t, 16>;
class ClientSession {
public:
    Send send;
    std::atomic<bool> ready{false};
    std::atomic<std::uint32_t> progress{0};
    std::atomic<bool> integrity{true};
    std::atomic<std::uint32_t> generation{0};
    void SetIdentity(const Identity& i) { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> l(mutex_); identity_ = EncodeIdentity(i); }
    void Reset() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> l(mutex_);
        ready = false; session_ = challenge_ = nextReport_ = 0; lastHello_ = lastResponse_ = lastSeen_ = 0;
        nonce_.clear(); queue_.clear(); helloSent_ = false; attempt_ = 0;
        ++generation;
    }
    bool Enqueue(const void* p, std::size_t n) {
        if (!p || n > MaxPayload) return false;
        Bytes b(static_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(p), static_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(p) + n);
        if (!ValidReport(b)) return false;
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> l(mutex_);
        for (auto& r : queue_) if (r.payload == b) return true;
        if (queue_.size() >= 256) return false;
        queue_.push_back({std::move(b)}); return true;
    }
    void Tick(std::uint32_t now) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> l(mutex_);
        if (session_ && Elapsed(now, lastSeen_) > TimeoutMs) {
            ready = false;
            // A temporary outage does not create a new server session. Preserve
            // report sequence numbers or the server would reject later reports.
            if (!helloSent_ || Elapsed(now, lastHello_) >= 3000) {
                send(Encode(Kind::Hello, 0, 0)); lastHello_ = now; helloSent_ = true;
            }
        }
        if (!session_) {
            if (!helloSent_ || Elapsed(now, lastHello_) >= 3000) {
                send(Encode(Kind::Hello, 0, 0)); lastHello_ = now; helloSent_ = true;
            }
            return;
        }
        if (!nonce_.empty() && !identity_.empty() && (!responseSent_ || Elapsed(now, lastResponse_) >= RetryMs(attempt_))) {
            Bytes payload = nonce_; payload.insert(payload.end(), identity_.begin(), identity_.end());
            Put32(payload, progress.load()); payload.push_back(integrity.load() ? 1 : 0);
            if (send(Encode(Kind::Response, session_, challenge_, payload))) {
                lastResponse_ = now; responseSent_ = true; if (attempt_ < 3) ++attempt_;
            }
        }
        if (!ready || queue_.empty() || (reportSent_ && Elapsed(now, lastReport_) < 63)) return;
        auto& r = queue_.front();
        if (!r.sent || Elapsed(now, r.lastSent) >= RetryMs(r.attempt)) {
            if (!r.sequence) r.sequence = ++nextReport_;
            if (send(Encode(Kind::Report, session_, r.sequence, r.payload))) {
                r.sent = true; r.lastSent = now; lastReport_ = now; reportSent_ = true; if (r.attempt < 3) ++r.attempt;
            }
        }
    }
    void Receive(const Frame& f, std::uint32_t now) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> l(mutex_);
        if (f.kind == Kind::Challenge && f.session && f.sequence && f.payload.size() == 16) {
            if (session_ != f.session) {
                session_ = f.session; ready = false; nextReport_ = 0;
                ++generation;
                for (auto& r : queue_) { r.sequence = 0; r.sent = false; r.attempt = 0; }
            }
            if (challenge_ && f.sequence < challenge_) return;
            if (f.sequence != challenge_ || nonce_ != f.payload) {
                challenge_ = f.sequence; nonce_ = f.payload; responseSent_ = false; attempt_ = 0;
            }
            lastSeen_ = now;
        } else if (f.kind == Kind::Accepted && f.session == session_ && session_ && f.sequence == challenge_ && f.payload.empty()) {
            ready = true; nonce_.clear(); lastSeen_ = now;
        } else if (f.kind == Kind::ReportAck && f.session == session_ && session_ && f.payload.empty() && !queue_.empty() && f.sequence == queue_.front().sequence) {
            queue_.pop_front(); lastSeen_ = now;
        }
    }
private:
    struct Pending { Bytes payload; std::uint32_t sequence = 0, lastSent = 0; unsigned attempt = 0; bool sent = false; };
    std::mutex mutex_;
    Bytes identity_, nonce_;
    std::deque<Pending> queue_;
    std::uint32_t session_ = 0, challenge_ = 0, nextReport_ = 0, lastHello_ = 0, lastResponse_ = 0, lastSeen_ = 0;
    bool helloSent_ = false, responseSent_ = false;
    unsigned attempt_ = 0;
    std::uint32_t lastReport_ = 0;
    bool reportSent_ = false;
};

class ServerSession {
public:
    Send send;
    std::function<bool(std::uint32_t&, Nonce&)> random;
    std::function<void(const Identity&)> connected;
    std::function<void(const Bytes&)> report;
    std::function<void(bool)> health; // false = grace expired, true = recovered
    std::function<bool()> canAccept; // wait until the game server has created this player
    bool ready = false;
    void Reset() {
        ready = false; session_ = challenge_ = accepted_ = 0; pending_ = false; timedOut_ = false;
        lastValid_ = started_ = lastSend_ = lastChallenge_ = 0; attempts_ = 0; reports_ = {};
        rateStart_ = 0; rateCount_ = 0; haveProgress_ = false;
        peerStarted_ = false;
    }
    void Tick(std::uint32_t now) {
        if (!peerStarted_) { peerStarted_ = true; started_ = lastValid_ = now; }
        if (Elapsed(now, lastValid_) > TimeoutMs && !timedOut_) {
            timedOut_ = true; if (health) health(false);
        }
        if (!session_) {
            if (!random || !random(session_, nonce_) || !session_) { session_ = 0; return; }
            StartChallenge(now);
        }
        if (!pending_ && Elapsed(now, lastChallenge_) >= HeartbeatMs) StartChallenge(now);
        if (pending_ && (attempts_ == 0 || Elapsed(now, lastSend_) >= RetryMs(attempts_ - 1))) SendChallenge(now);
    }
    void Receive(const Frame& f, std::uint32_t now) {
        if (Elapsed(now, rateStart_) >= 1000) { rateStart_ = now; rateCount_ = 0; }
        if (++rateCount_ > 64) return;
        if (f.kind == Kind::Hello && !f.session && !f.sequence && f.payload.empty()) {
            if (!session_) {
                if (!random || !random(session_, nonce_) || !session_) { session_ = 0; return; }
                if (!peerStarted_) { peerStarted_ = true; started_ = lastValid_ = now; }
                StartChallenge(now);
            }
            if (pending_ && (!attempts_ || Elapsed(now, lastSend_) >= 1000)) SendChallenge(now);
            return;
        }
        if (!session_ || f.session != session_) return;
        if (f.kind == Kind::Response) {
            if (f.payload.size() != 99 || f.sequence != challenge_ || !std::equal(nonce_.begin(), nonce_.end(), f.payload.begin())) return;
            Identity identity;
            if (!DecodeIdentity(Bytes(f.payload.begin() + 16, f.payload.begin() + 94), identity) || identity.major != 9 || identity.minor < 11) return;
            if (f.payload[98] != 1) return; // code integrity failed; do not renew lease
            if (canAccept && !canAccept()) return;
            auto progress = Get32(f.payload.data() + 94);
            if (!haveProgress_ || progress != progress_) { progress_ = progress; progressAt_ = now; haveProgress_ = true; }
            if (Elapsed(now, progressAt_) > TimeoutMs) return;
            bool first = !ready;
            ready = true; pending_ = false; accepted_ = f.sequence; lastValid_ = now;
            send(Encode(Kind::Accepted, session_, accepted_));
            if (first && connected) connected(identity);
            if (timedOut_) { timedOut_ = false; if (health) health(true); }
            return;
        }
        if (f.kind == Kind::Report && ready && !timedOut_ && ValidReport(f.payload)) {
            if (reports_.Duplicate(f.sequence)) { send(Encode(Kind::ReportAck, session_, f.sequence)); return; }
            if (!reports_.Next(f.sequence)) return;
            reports_.last = f.sequence;
            if (report) report(f.payload);
            send(Encode(Kind::ReportAck, session_, f.sequence));
        }
    }
private:
    void StartChallenge(std::uint32_t now) {
        std::uint32_t unused = 0;
        if (!random || !random(unused, nonce_)) return;
        ++challenge_; if (!challenge_) ++challenge_;
        pending_ = true; attempts_ = 0; lastChallenge_ = now;
    }
    void SendChallenge(std::uint32_t now) {
        send(Encode(Kind::Challenge, session_, challenge_, Bytes(nonce_.begin(), nonce_.end())));
        lastSend_ = now; if (attempts_ < 4) ++attempts_;
    }
    Nonce nonce_{};
    ReportSequence reports_;
    std::uint32_t session_ = 0, challenge_ = 0, accepted_ = 0, lastValid_ = 0, started_ = 0, lastSend_ = 0, lastChallenge_ = 0;
    std::uint32_t rateStart_ = 0, progress_ = 0, progressAt_ = 0;
    unsigned attempts_ = 0, rateCount_ = 0;
    bool pending_ = false, timedOut_ = false, haveProgress_ = false;
    bool peerStarted_ = false;
};
}

```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/common/types.hpp`:

```hpp
/*
    This is a KeyListener project file
    Developer: CyberMor <cyber.mor.2020@gmail.com>

    See more here https://github.com/CyberMor/keylistener

    Copyright (c) Daniel (CyberMor) 2020 All rights reserved
*/

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstddef>
#include <climits>

// ----------------------------------------------------------------

// requirement : byte = 8 bits
// requirement : word = 2 bytes

using sbyte_t = std::int8_t;    // signed byte
using ubyte_t = std::uint8_t;   // unsigned byte

using sword_t = std::int16_t;   // signed word
using uword_t = std::uint16_t;  // unsigned word

using sdword_t = std::int32_t;  // signed double word
using udword_t = std::uint32_t; // unsigned double word

using sqword_t = std::int64_t;  // signed quad word
using uqword_t = std::uint64_t; // unsigned quad word

using fdword_t = float;         // floating double word
using fqword_t = double;        // floating quad word

// ----------------------------------------------------------------

#ifndef CHAR_BIT
#error failed to determine byte size
#endif

static_assert(CHAR_BIT == 8, "invalid 'byte' size");

// ----------------------------------------------------------------

static_assert(sizeof(sbyte_t) == 1, "invalid 'sbyte_t' size");
static_assert(sizeof(ubyte_t) == 1, "invalid 'ubyte_t' size");

static_assert(sizeof(sword_t) == 2, "invalid 'sword_t' size");
static_assert(sizeof(uword_t) == 2, "invalid 'uword_t' size");

static_assert(sizeof(sdword_t) == 4, "invalid 'sdword_t' size");
static_assert(sizeof(udword_t) == 4, "invalid 'udword_t' size");

static_assert(sizeof(sqword_t) == 8, "invalid 'sqword_t' size");
static_assert(sizeof(uqword_t) == 8, "invalid 'uqword_t' size");

static_assert(sizeof(fdword_t) == 4, "invalid 'fdword_t' size");
static_assert(sizeof(fqword_t) == 8, "invalid 'fqword_t' size");

// ----------------------------------------------------------------

using sint_t = signed int;        // signed platform-specific word
using uint_t = unsigned int;      // unsigned platform-specific word

using size_t = std::size_t;       // platform-specific size type (unsigned)

using  ptr_t = void*;             // universal pointer type
using cptr_t = const void*;       // const universal pointer type

using  adr_t = ubyte_t*;          // memory address type
using cadr_t = const ubyte_t*;    // const memory address type

using  str_t = char*;             // string type
using cstr_t = const char*;       // const string type

```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/common/unprotect_scope.hpp`:

```hpp
/*
    This is a KeyListener project file
    Developer: CyberMor <cyber.mor.2020@gmail.com>

    See more here https://github.com/CyberMor/keylistener

    Copyright (c) Daniel (CyberMor) 2020 All rights reserved
*/

#pragma once

#include <cassert>

#include "types.hpp"

#ifdef _WIN32
#include <Windows.h>
#else
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#endif

template <size_t Size>
struct UnprotectScope {

    static_assert(Size > 0);

public:

    UnprotectScope() noexcept = default;
    ~UnprotectScope() noexcept
    {
        Disable();
    }

    UnprotectScope(const UnprotectScope&) = delete;
    UnprotectScope(UnprotectScope&& object) noexcept
        : _address { object._address }
        , _enabled { object._enabled }
#ifdef _WIN32
        , _protect { object._protect }
#endif
    {
        object._address = nullptr;
        object._enabled = false;
    }

    UnprotectScope& operator=(const UnprotectScope&) = delete;
    UnprotectScope& operator=(UnprotectScope&& object) noexcept
    {
        if (&object != this)
        {
            Disable();

            _address = object._address;
            _enabled = object._enabled;
#ifdef _WIN32
            _protect = object._protect;
#endif

            object._address = nullptr;
            object._enabled = false;
        }

        return *this;
    }

public:

    UnprotectScope(const ptr_t address, const bool enabled = true) noexcept
        : _address { address }
    {
        if (enabled) Enable();
    }

public:

    bool Valid() const noexcept
    {
        return _address != nullptr;
    }

    bool Invalid() const noexcept
    {
        return _address == nullptr;
    }

public:

    bool Initialize(const ptr_t address, const bool enabled = true) noexcept
    {
        Disable();

        _address = address;
#ifdef _WIN32
        _protect = PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE;
#endif

        if (enabled) Enable();

        return _address != nullptr &&
            (enabled == false || _enabled == true);
    }

    void Deinitialize() noexcept
    {
        Disable();

        _address = nullptr;
    }

public:

    void Enable() noexcept
    {
        if (_enabled == false)
        {
            assert(_address != nullptr);
#ifdef _WIN32
            assert(_protect == PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE);

            if (VirtualProtect(_address, Size, _protect, &_protect) != 0)
                _enabled = true;
#else
            if (const long page_size = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE); page_size != -1)
            {
                const size_t begin = (size_t)(_address) & ~((size_t)(page_size) - 1);
                const size_t end   = (size_t)(_address) + Size;

                if (mprotect((ptr_t)(begin), end - begin, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE | PROT_EXEC) == 0)
                    _enabled = true;
            }
#endif
        }
    }

    void Disable() noexcept
    {
        if (_enabled == true)
        {
            assert(_address != nullptr);
#ifdef _WIN32
            if (VirtualProtect(_address, Size, _protect, &_protect) != 0)
                _enabled = false;
#endif
        }
    }

public:

    ptr_t Address() const noexcept
    {
        return _address;
    }

    constexpr size_t Length() const noexcept
    {
        return Size;
    }

private:

    ptr_t _address = nullptr;
    bool  _enabled = false;
#ifdef _WIN32
    DWORD _protect = PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE;
#endif

};

inline bool UnprotectMemory(const ptr_t address, const size_t length) noexcept
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    if (DWORD protect; VirtualProtect(address, length, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE, &protect) != 0)
        return true;
#else
    if (const long page_size = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE); page_size != -1)
    {
        const size_t begin = (size_t)(address) & ~((size_t)(page_size) - 1);
        const size_t end   = (size_t)(address) + length;

        if (mprotect((ptr_t)(begin), end - begin, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE | PROT_EXEC) == 0)
            return true;
    }
#endif
    return false;
}
```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/common/version.hpp`:

```hpp
/*
    This is a KeyListener project file
    Developer: CyberMor <cyber.mor.2020@gmail.com>

    See more here https://github.com/CyberMor/keylistener

    Copyright (c) Daniel (CyberMor) 2020 All rights reserved
*/

#pragma once

#include "types.hpp"

// ----------------------------------------------------------------

using Version = udword_t;

// ----------------------------------------------------------------

using VersionMajor = ubyte_t;
using VersionMinor = ubyte_t;
using VersionPatch = uword_t;

// ----------------------------------------------------------------

constexpr VersionMajor GetVersionMajor(const Version version) noexcept
{
    return static_cast<VersionMajor>(version >> 24);
}

constexpr VersionMinor GetVersionMinor(const Version version) noexcept
{
    return static_cast<VersionMinor>(version >> 16 & 0xFF);
}

constexpr VersionPatch GetVersionPatch(const Version version) noexcept
{
    return static_cast<VersionPatch>(version & 0xFFFF);
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------

constexpr Version MakeVersion(const VersionMajor major,
                              const VersionMinor minor,
                              const VersionPatch patch) noexcept
{
    return static_cast<Version>(major) << 24 |
           static_cast<Version>(minor) << 16 |
           static_cast<Version>(patch);
}
```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/common/view.hpp`:

```hpp
/*
    This is a KeyListener project file
    Developer: CyberMor <cyber.mor.2020@gmail.com>

    See more here https://github.com/CyberMor/keylistener

    Copyright (c) Daniel (CyberMor) 2020 All rights reserved
*/

#pragma once

#include <cassert>

#include "types.hpp"

template <class DataType, class SizeType = size_t>
struct View {

    constexpr View() noexcept = default;
    ~View() noexcept = default;
    constexpr View(const View&) noexcept = default;
    constexpr View(View&&) noexcept = default;
    constexpr View& operator=(const View&) noexcept = default;
    constexpr View& operator=(View&&) noexcept = default;

public:

    constexpr View(DataType* const data, const SizeType size) noexcept
        : data { data }
        , size { size }
    {
        assert(size >= 0);
        assert(size == 0 || data != nullptr);
    }

public:

    template <class Buffer, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_pod_v<std::remove_reference_t<Buffer>>>>
    static constexpr View FromBuffer(Buffer&& buffer) noexcept
    {
        static_assert(sizeof(Buffer) % sizeof(DataType) == 0);

        return { reinterpret_cast<DataType*>(&buffer), sizeof(Buffer) / sizeof(DataType) };
    }

    template <class MemoryDataType, class MemorySizeType>
    static constexpr View FromMemory(const View<MemoryDataType, MemorySizeType>& memory) noexcept
    {
        assert(memory.Bytes() % sizeof(DataType) == 0);

        return { reinterpret_cast<DataType*> (memory.Data()),
                      static_cast<SizeType>  (memory.Bytes() / sizeof(DataType)) };
    }

public:

    constexpr bool operator<(const View& view) const noexcept
    {
        return data < view.data || data == view.data && size < view.size;
    }

    constexpr bool operator>(const View& view) const noexcept
    {
        return data > view.data || data == view.data && size > view.size;
    }

    constexpr bool operator<=(const View& view) const noexcept
    {
        return data < view.data || data == view.data && size <= view.size;
    }

    constexpr bool operator>=(const View& view) const noexcept
    {
        return data > view.data || data == view.data && size >= view.size;
    }

    constexpr bool operator==(const View& view) const noexcept
    {
        return data == view.data && size == view.size;
    }

    constexpr bool operator!=(const View& view) const noexcept
    {
        return data != view.data || size != view.size;
    }

public:

    constexpr const DataType& operator[](const SizeType index) const noexcept
    {
        assert(data != nullptr);
        assert(index >= 0 && index < size);
        return data[index];
    }

    constexpr DataType& operator[](const SizeType index) noexcept
    {
        assert(data != nullptr);
        assert(index >= 0 && index < size);
        return data[index];
    }

public:

    constexpr bool Valid() const noexcept
    {
        return data != nullptr;
    }

    constexpr bool Invalid() const noexcept
    {
        return data == nullptr;
    }

public:

    constexpr bool Contains(const View& view) const noexcept
    {
        return view.data >= data && view.data + view.size <= data + size;
    }

    constexpr bool Intersect(const View& view) const noexcept
    {
        return view.data <= data + size && view.data + view.size >= data;
    }

public:

    constexpr View SubLeft(SizeType offset) const noexcept
    {
        assert(offset >= 0);

        if (offset > size) offset = size;

        return { data + offset, size - offset };
    }

    constexpr View SubLeft(SizeType offset, SizeType length) const noexcept
    {
        assert(offset >= 0 && length >= 0);

        if (offset > size) offset = size;

        if (const SizeType available = size - offset; length > available)
            length = available;

        return { data + offset, length };
    }

public:

    constexpr View SubRight(SizeType offset) const noexcept
    {
        assert(offset >= 0);

        if (offset > size) offset = size;

        return { data, size - offset };
    }

    constexpr View SubRight(SizeType offset, SizeType length) const noexcept
    {
        assert(offset >= 0 && length >= 0);

        if (offset > size) offset = size;

        if (const SizeType available = size - offset; length > available)
            length = available;

        return { (data + size) - (offset + length), length };
    }

public:

    constexpr SizeType Bytes() const noexcept
    {
        return size * sizeof(DataType);
    }

public:

    constexpr DataType* First() const noexcept
    {
        assert(data != nullptr);
        assert(size != 0);
        return Data();
    }

    constexpr DataType* Last() const noexcept
    {
        assert(data != nullptr);
        assert(size != 0);
        return Data() + Size() - 1;
    }

public:

    constexpr DataType* Begin() const noexcept
    {
        return Data();
    }

    constexpr DataType* End() const noexcept
    {
        return nullptr;
    }

public:

    constexpr DataType* Previous(DataType* const iterator) const noexcept
    {
        return iterator != Data() ? (iterator != nullptr ? iterator - 1 : Data() + Size() - 1) : nullptr;
    }

    constexpr DataType* Next(DataType* const iterator) const noexcept
    {
        return iterator != Data() + Size() - 1 ? (iterator != nullptr ? iterator + 1 : Data()) : nullptr;
    }

public:

    constexpr DataType* Data() const noexcept
    {
        return data;
    }

    constexpr SizeType Size() const noexcept
    {
        return size;
    }

public:

    DataType* data = nullptr;
    SizeType  size = 0;

};
```

```inc
    #endinput
```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/server/main.cpp`:

```cpp
// main_server.cpp - Anti-Cheat Server Plugin v1.5.2+ 
// รองรับ Client v9.10.0 (HWID_V2) + Client เวอร์ชันเก่า + Dynamic File Report

#include <set>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <version.hpp>
#include "pawn.hpp"
#include "network.hpp"
#include <session.hpp>
#include <chrono>
#include <array>
#ifdef _WIN32
#include <wincrypt.h>
#pragma comment(lib, "advapi32.lib")
#else
#include <cstdio>
#endif

constexpr Version kServerRequiredVersion = MakeVersion(9, 10, 0);

constexpr ubyte_t kPacketHandshake = 250;
constexpr ubyte_t kPacketCheatDetected = 251;
constexpr ubyte_t kPacketRequestHandshake = 252;

extern void* pAMXFunctions;
static std::set<AMX*> g_AmxInstances;

static void (*logprintf)(const char* format, ...);

static std::array<stp::ServerSession, 1000> g_Sessions;
static std::array<std::uint32_t, 1000> g_JoinTime{};
static std::uint32_t Now() { return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now().time_since_epoch()).count()); }
static bool RandomNonce(std::uint32_t& session, stp::Nonce& nonce) {
#ifdef _WIN32
    HCRYPTPROV provider = 0;
    if (!CryptAcquireContextA(&provider, nullptr, nullptr, PROV_RSA_FULL, CRYPT_VERIFYCONTEXT)) return false;
    bool ok = CryptGenRandom(provider, sizeof(session), reinterpret_cast<BYTE*>(&session)) && CryptGenRandom(provider, static_cast<DWORD>(nonce.size()), nonce.data());
    CryptReleaseContext(provider, 0); return ok;
#else
    auto file = std::fopen("/dev/urandom", "rb"); if (!file) return false;
    bool ok = std::fread(&session, 1, sizeof(session), file) == sizeof(session) && std::fread(nonce.data(), 1, nonce.size(), file) == nonce.size();
    std::fclose(file); return ok;
#endif
}

// A RakNet connection can exist before OnPlayerConnect. Delay acceptance until
// IsPlayerConnected says the SA-MP player exists, avoiding an early callback
// being erased by gamemode initialization. Resolve the registered Pawn native.
static bool GamePlayerReady(uword_t player) {
    bool nativeFound = false;
    for (auto amx : g_AmxInstances) {
        int index = -1;
        if (amx_FindNative(amx, "IsPlayerConnected", &index) != AMX_ERR_NONE || !amx->base) continue;
        nativeFound = true;
        auto header = reinterpret_cast<const AMX_HEADER*>(amx->base);
        if (index < 0 || header->defsize < sizeof(ucell) || header->natives < 0 || header->libraries > header->size) continue;
        auto offset = header->natives + index * header->defsize;
        if (offset < header->natives || offset + int(sizeof(ucell)) > header->libraries) continue;
        ucell address = 0;
        std::memcpy(&address, amx->base + offset, sizeof(address));
        if (!address) continue;
        cell params[]{sizeof(cell), player};
        if (reinterpret_cast<AMX_NATIVE>(address)(amx, params)) return true;
    }
    // A script which never imports IsPlayerConnected cannot expose its native.
    return !nativeFound && Now() - g_JoinTime[player] >= 1500;
}

// ====================== Callback Functions ======================

static void CallPawnCallback(const char* funcName, int playerid, int value)
{
    int idx;
    for (AMX* amx : g_AmxInstances)
    {
        if (amx_FindPublic(amx, funcName, &idx) == AMX_ERR_NONE)
        {
            amx_Push(amx, static_cast<cell>(value));
            amx_Push(amx, static_cast<cell>(playerid));
            amx_Exec(amx, nullptr, idx);
        }
    }
}

static void CallPawnCallbackStr(const char* funcName, int playerid, const std::string& strVal)
{
    int idx;
    for (AMX* amx : g_AmxInstances)
    {
        if (amx_FindPublic(amx, funcName, &idx) == AMX_ERR_NONE)
        {
            cell amx_addr, * phys_addr;
            amx_PushString(amx, &amx_addr, &phys_addr, strVal.c_str(), 0, 0);
            amx_Push(amx, static_cast<cell>(playerid));
            amx_Exec(amx, nullptr, idx);
            amx_Release(amx, amx_addr);
        }
    }
}

// ====================== NEW: Dynamic File Callback ======================
static void CallPawnCallbackDynamic(const char* funcName, int playerid, int fileType,
    const std::string& filename, const std::string& md5hash)
{
    int idx;
    for (AMX* amx : g_AmxInstances)
    {
        if (amx_FindPublic(amx, funcName, &idx) == AMX_ERR_NONE)
        {
            cell amx_addr1, * phys_addr1, amx_addr2, * phys_addr2;

            amx_PushString(amx, &amx_addr2, &phys_addr2, md5hash.c_str(), 0, 0);
            amx_PushString(amx, &amx_addr1, &phys_addr1, filename.c_str(), 0, 0);
            amx_Push(amx, static_cast<cell>(fileType));
            amx_Push(amx, static_cast<cell>(playerid));

            amx_Exec(amx, nullptr, idx);

            amx_Release(amx, amx_addr2);
            amx_Release(amx, amx_addr1);
        }
    }
}

// ====================== Packet Handler ======================

static bool DispatchReport(const uword_t player, const cptr_t data, const uint_t size) noexcept
{
    if (size < 1 || data == nullptr) return false;

    const ubyte_t packetId = static_cast<const ubyte_t*>(data)[0];

    // ==================== Cheat Detected ====================
    if (packetId == kPacketCheatDetected)
    {
        if (size < 2) return false;

        int cheatId = static_cast<int>(static_cast<const ubyte_t*>(data)[1]);

        if (cheatId == 200 && size > 2) // Injected Module
        {
            if (size < 4) return false;
            ubyte_t strLen = static_cast<const ubyte_t*>(data)[2];
            if (size < 3 + strLen) return false;

            std::string modulePath(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(data) + 3, strLen);
            CallPawnCallbackStr("OnPlayerDetectedInjectedModule", static_cast<int>(player), modulePath);
            return true;
        }

        else if (cheatId == 210 || cheatId == 220) // New Injected Module (Runtime)
        {
            if (size < 4) return false;
            ubyte_t strLen = static_cast<const ubyte_t*>(data)[2];
            if (size < 3 + strLen) return false;

            std::string modulePath(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(data) + 3, strLen);
            CallPawnCallbackStr("OnPlayerDetectedNewInjectedModule", static_cast<int>(player), modulePath);
            return true;
        }

        // ==================== DYNAMIC FILE REPORT (205) ====================
        else if (cheatId == 205)
        {
            if (size < 20) return false;

            const ubyte_t fileType = static_cast<const ubyte_t*>(data)[2];

            const ubyte_t* md5Bytes = static_cast<const ubyte_t*>(data) + 3;
            ubyte_t nameLen = static_cast<const ubyte_t*>(data)[19];

            if (size < 20 + nameLen) return false;

            std::string filename(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(data) + 20, nameLen);

            // แปลง MD5 16 bytes เป็น Hex String
            char md5Hex[33] = { 0 };
            for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) {
                sprintf(md5Hex + (i * 2), "%02X", md5Bytes[i]);
            }

            CallPawnCallbackDynamic("OnPlayerDetectedDynamicFile",
                static_cast<int>(player),
                static_cast<int>(fileType),
                filename,
                std::string(md5Hex));

            return true;
        }

        // การตรวจจับปกติ (Static Cheat)
        if (size == 2)
        {
            CallPawnCallback("OnPlayerDetectedIllegalFile", static_cast<int>(player), cheatId);
            return true;
        }
    }

    return false;
}

static bool OnPacket(uword_t player, cptr_t data, uint_t size) noexcept {
    if (!data || size < 4) return false;
    auto p = static_cast<const ubyte_t*>(data);
    if (p[0] != stp::PacketId || p[1] != 'S' || p[2] != 'T' || p[3] != 'P') return false;
    if (player >= g_Sessions.size()) return true;
    try {
        stp::Frame frame;
        if (stp::Decode(data, size, frame)) g_Sessions[player].Receive(frame, Now());
    } catch (...) { logprintf("[STP Vanguard] Packet processing failed for %u", unsigned(player)); }
    return true;
}

PLUGIN_EXPORT void PLUGIN_CALL ProcessTick() noexcept {
    static std::uint32_t last = 0;
    auto now = Now();
    if (stp::Elapsed(now, last) < 100) return;
    last = now;
    for (uword_t player = 0; player < g_Sessions.size(); ++player) {
        if (Network::Instance().IsPlayerConnected(player)) g_Sessions[player].Tick(now);
    }
}

// ====================== Plugin Load ======================

PLUGIN_EXPORT bool PLUGIN_CALL Load(void** ppData) noexcept
{
    pAMXFunctions = ppData[PLUGIN_DATA_AMX_EXPORTS];
    logprintf = reinterpret_cast<void(*)(const char*, ...)>(ppData[PLUGIN_DATA_LOGPRINTF]);

    for (uword_t player = 0; player < g_Sessions.size(); ++player) {
        auto& session = g_Sessions[player];
        session.Reset(); session.random = RandomNonce;
        session.canAccept = [player] { return GamePlayerReady(player); };
        session.send = [player](const stp::Bytes& b) { return Network::Instance().SendPacketToPlayer(player, b.data(), static_cast<uint_t>(b.size())); };
        session.connected = [player](const stp::Identity& i) {
            CallPawnCallback("OnPlayerAntiCheatConnect", player, 1);
            CallPawnCallback("OnPlayerAntiCheatType", player, i.type);
            CallPawnCallbackStr("OnPlayerCheckHwid", player, i.hwid);
            CallPawnCallbackStr("OnPlayerCheckHwidV2", player, i.hwid2);
        };
        session.report = [player](const stp::Bytes& b) { DispatchReport(player, b.data(), static_cast<uint_t>(b.size())); };
        session.health = [player](bool healthy) {
            logprintf("[STP Vanguard] Player %u channel %s", unsigned(player), healthy ? "recovered" : "timed out");
            CallPawnCallback(healthy ? "OnPlayerAntiCheatRecovered" : "OnPlayerAntiCheatTimeout", player, healthy ? 1 : 0);
        };
    }
    Network::Instance().OnDisconnect = [](uword_t player) {
        if (player < g_Sessions.size()) { g_Sessions[player].Reset(); g_JoinTime[player] = Now(); }
    };
    Network::Instance().OnPacket = OnPacket;

    if (!Network::Instance().Initialize(ppData[PLUGIN_DATA_LOGPRINTF]))
    {
        logprintf("[Anti-Cheat] ERROR: Network initialization failed!");
        return false;
    }

    logprintf("=============================================================");
    logprintf("STP Vanguard 9.11.0 LOADED (request/response/ACK)");
    logprintf("Requires client 9.11.0; heartbeat 10s; grace 45s; no sampvoice dependency");
    logprintf("By FocusGamerCH");
    logprintf("=============================================================");

    return true;
}

PLUGIN_EXPORT void PLUGIN_CALL Unload() noexcept
{
    Network::Instance().Deinitialize();
    g_AmxInstances.clear();
}

PLUGIN_EXPORT int PLUGIN_CALL AmxLoad(AMX* amx) noexcept
{
    g_AmxInstances.insert(amx);
    // Reloaded gamemodes initialize their player state again. Start fresh
    // handshakes so the new script receives its initial callbacks as well.
    for (auto& session : g_Sessions) session.Reset();
    return AMX_ERR_NONE;
}

PLUGIN_EXPORT int PLUGIN_CALL AmxUnload(AMX* amx) noexcept
{
    g_AmxInstances.erase(amx);
    return AMX_ERR_NONE;
}

PLUGIN_EXPORT unsigned int PLUGIN_CALL Supports() noexcept
{
    return SUPPORTS_VERSION | SUPPORTS_AMX_NATIVES | SUPPORTS_PROCESS_TICK;
}
```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/server/network.hpp`:

```hpp
/*
    This is a KeyListener project file
    Developer: CyberMor <cyber.mor.2020@gmail.com>

    See more here https://github.com/CyberMor/keylistener

    Copyright (c) Daniel (CyberMor) 2020 All rights reserved
*/

#pragma once

#include <functional>
#include <array>
#include <cstdint>

#include <types.hpp>
#include <unprotect_scope.hpp>
#include <jump_hook.hpp>
#include <scanner.hpp>

#include "raknet/bitstream.h"
#include "raknet/networktypes.h"  // ใช้ชื่อนี้ตาม project ของคุณ

#ifdef _WIN32
#define STDCALL  __stdcall
#define THISCALL __thiscall
#else
#define STDCALL
#define THISCALL
#endif

#ifdef _WIN32
constexpr size_t kRaknetReceiveOffset = 10;
constexpr size_t kRaknetDeallocatePacketOffset = 12;
constexpr size_t kRaknetSendOffset = 7; // SA-MP 0.3.7 ABI, verified against Pawn.RakNet
#else
// Linux offsets (ถ้าใช้ Linux ปรับตามจริง)
constexpr size_t kRaknetReceiveOffset = 11;
constexpr size_t kRaknetDeallocatePacketOffset = 13;
constexpr size_t kRaknetSendOffset = 9;
#endif

typedef Packet* (THISCALL* raknet_receive_t)          (ptr_t);
typedef void    (THISCALL* raknet_deallocatepacket_t) (ptr_t, Packet*);
typedef bool    (THISCALL* raknet_send_t)             (ptr_t, BitStream*, PacketPriority, PacketReliability, char, PlayerID, bool);


struct Network {

    Network(const Network&) = delete;
    Network(Network&&) = delete;
    Network& operator=(const Network&) = delete;
    Network& operator=(Network&&) = delete;

private:

    Network() noexcept = default;
    ~Network() noexcept = default;

public:

    static Network& Instance() noexcept
    {
        static Network instance;
        return instance;
    }

public:

    bool Initialize(const cptr_t base) noexcept
    {
#ifdef _WIN32
        static const char kGetRakServerPattern[] =
            "\x64\xA1\x00\x00\x00\x00\x50\x64\x89\x25\x00\x00\x00\x00\x51\x68\x18\x0E\x00\x00\xE8"
            "\xFF\xFF\xFF\xFF\x83\xC4\x04\x89\x04\x24\x85\xC0\xC7\x44\x24\xFF\x00\x00\x00\x00\x74\x16";
        static const char kGetRakServerMask[] =
            "xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx????x????xxxxxxxxxxx?xxxxxx";
#else
        static const char kGetRakServerPattern[] =
            "\x04\x24\xFF\xFF\xFF\xFF\x89\x75\xFF\x89\x5D\xFF\xE8\xFF\xFF\xFF\xFF\x89\x04\x24\x89"
            "\xC6\xE8\xFF\xFF\xFF\xFF\x89\xF0\x8B\x5D\xFF\x8B\x75\xFF\x89\xEC\x5D\xC3";
        static const char kGetRakServerMask[] =
            "xx????xx?xx?x????xxxxxx????xxxx?xx?xxxx";
#endif

        const auto [address, length] = GetModuleInfo(base);
        if (address == nullptr) return false;

        const auto pointer = Scanner(address, length).Find(kGetRakServerPattern, kGetRakServerMask);
        if (pointer == nullptr) return false;

        return _hook_getrakserver.Initialize(static_cast<adr_t>(pointer) - 7, GetRakServerHook);
    }

    void Deinitialize() noexcept
    {
        if (_rakserver && _receive) {
            auto table = *static_cast<ptr_t**>(_rakserver);
            if (table[kRaknetReceiveOffset] == reinterpret_cast<ptr_t>(ReceiveHook)) {
                const UnprotectScope<sizeof(ptr_t)> scope{table + kRaknetReceiveOffset};
                table[kRaknetReceiveOffset] = reinterpret_cast<ptr_t>(_receive);
            }
        }
        _hook_getrakserver.Deinitialize();
        _rakserver = nullptr;
    }
    bool SendPacketToPlayer(uword_t player, const void* data, uint_t size) noexcept
    {
        if (!_rakserver || !_send || player >= endpoints.size() || !endpoints[player].online || !data || !size) return false;
        BitStream stream;
        stream.Write(static_cast<const char*>(data), size);
        return _send(_rakserver, &stream, HIGH_PRIORITY, RELIABLE_ORDERED, 7, endpoints[player].id, false);
    }
    bool IsPlayerConnected(uword_t player) const noexcept { return player < endpoints.size() && endpoints[player].online; }

private:

#ifdef _WIN32
    static Packet* __fastcall ReceiveHook(const ptr_t, void*) noexcept
#else
    static Packet* ReceiveHook(const ptr_t) noexcept
#endif
    {
        for (unsigned consumed = 0; consumed < 64; ++consumed) {
            Packet* packet = Instance().Receive();
            Instance().TrackEndpoint(packet);
            if (!packet || !Instance().OnPacket ||
                !Instance().OnPacket(packet->playerIndex, packet->data, packet->length)) return packet;
            Instance().DeallocatePacket(packet);
        }
        return nullptr;
    }

private:

    static ptr_t STDCALL GetRakServerHook() noexcept
    {
        const auto GetRakServer = Instance()._hook_getrakserver.Address();
        Instance()._hook_getrakserver.Deinitialize();

        Instance()._rakserver = reinterpret_cast<ptr_t(*)()>(GetRakServer)();
        if (Instance()._rakserver != nullptr)
        {
            const auto vtable = *static_cast<ptr_t**>(Instance()._rakserver);

            Instance()._receive = reinterpret_cast<raknet_receive_t>(vtable[kRaknetReceiveOffset]);
            Instance()._deallocatepacket = reinterpret_cast<raknet_deallocatepacket_t>(vtable[kRaknetDeallocatePacketOffset]);
            Instance()._send = reinterpret_cast<raknet_send_t>(vtable[kRaknetSendOffset]);

            const UnprotectScope<sizeof(ptr_t)> scope{ vtable + kRaknetReceiveOffset };
            vtable[kRaknetReceiveOffset] = reinterpret_cast<ptr_t>(ReceiveHook);
        }

        return Instance()._rakserver;
    }

private:

    Packet* Receive() noexcept
    {
        return _receive(_rakserver);
    }

    void DeallocatePacket(Packet* const packet) noexcept
    {
        _deallocatepacket(_rakserver, packet);
    }

public:

    std::function<bool(uword_t, cptr_t, uint_t)> OnPacket;
    std::function<void(uword_t)> OnDisconnect;

private:

    ptr_t _rakserver = nullptr;

    raknet_receive_t _receive = nullptr;
    raknet_deallocatepacket_t _deallocatepacket = nullptr;
    raknet_send_t _send = nullptr;  // เพิ่ม


    struct Endpoint { PlayerID id{}; bool online = false; };
    std::array<Endpoint, 1000> endpoints{};
    void TrackEndpoint(const Packet* packet) {
        if (!packet || !packet->data || !packet->length || packet->playerIndex >= endpoints.size()) return;
        auto& endpoint = endpoints[packet->playerIndex];
        auto id = packet->data[0];
        if (id == 21 || id == 22) {
            endpoint.online = false;
            if (OnDisconnect) OnDisconnect(packet->playerIndex);
            return;
        }
        // Endpoint belongs to the actual received RakNet packet, never guessed vtable slots.
        if (!endpoint.online || id == 19 || endpoint.id.binaryAddress != packet->playerId.binaryAddress || endpoint.id.port != packet->playerId.port) {
            if (OnDisconnect) OnDisconnect(packet->playerIndex);
            endpoint.id = packet->playerId; endpoint.online = true;
        }
    }
    JumpHook _hook_getrakserver;

};

```

`AC_STP (nocleo)/server/pawn.hpp`:

```hpp
/*
    This is a KeyListener project file
    Developer: CyberMor <cyber.mor.2020@gmail.com>

    See more here https://github.com/CyberMor/keylistener

    Copyright (c) Daniel (CyberMor) 2020 All rights reserved
*/

#pragma once

#include <vector>

#include <types.hpp>

#include "pawn/amx/amx.h"
#include "pawn/plugincommon.h"

#include "raknet/networktypes.h"

struct AmxScript;

extern std::vector<AmxScript> gScripts;

struct AmxScript {

    AmxScript() = delete;
    ~AmxScript() noexcept = default;
    AmxScript(const AmxScript&) noexcept = default;
    AmxScript(AmxScript&&) noexcept = default;
    AmxScript& operator=(const AmxScript&) noexcept = default;
    AmxScript& operator=(AmxScript&&) noexcept = default;

public:

    AmxScript(AMX* const amx, const int onplayerkeydown, const int onplayerkeyup) noexcept
        : _amx             { amx }
        , _onplayerkeydown { onplayerkeydown }
        , _onplayerkeyup   { onplayerkeyup }
    {}

public:

    static bool Register(AMX* const amx) noexcept
    {
        int onplayerkeydown;
        int onplayerkeyup;

        if (amx_FindPublic(amx, "OnPlayerKeyDown", &onplayerkeydown) != 0 ||
            amx_FindPublic(amx, "OnPlayerKeyUp", &onplayerkeyup) != 0)
            return false;

        gScripts.emplace_back(amx, onplayerkeydown, onplayerkeyup);

        return true;
    }

public:

    void OnPlayerKeyDown(const uword_t player, const ubyte_t key) noexcept
    {
        amx_Push(_amx, static_cast<cell>(key));
        amx_Push(_amx, static_cast<cell>(player));
        amx_Exec(_amx, nullptr, _onplayerkeydown);
    }

    void OnPlayerKeyUp(const uword_t player, const ubyte_t key) noexcept
    {
        amx_Push(_amx, static_cast<cell>(key));
        amx_Push(_amx, static_cast<cell>(player));
        amx_Exec(_amx, nullptr, _onplayerkeyup);
    }

private:

    AMX* _amx;

private:

    int _onplayerkeydown;
    int _onplayerkeyup;

};

inline std::vector<AmxScript> gScripts;

inline void OnPlayerKeyDown(const uword_t player, const ubyte_t key) noexcept
{
    for (auto& script : gScripts) script.OnPlayerKeyDown(player, key);
}

inline void OnPlayerKeyUp(const uword_t player, const ubyte_t key) noexcept
{
    for (auto& script : gScripts) script.OnPlayerKeyUp(player, key);
}

```

```h
/*  Pawn Abstract Machine (for the Pawn language)
```
